Takeaways from Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2","published_date":"2026-05-07","author_name":"Lance Concannon","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Marketing"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afyuk8BOoF08xufV_BlogImageday2.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=0,0,1920,1280\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"afyuk8BOoF08xufV","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afyuk8BOoF08xufV_BlogImageday2.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=320,0,1280,1280\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afyuk8BOoF08xufV","edit":{"x":320,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"As Meltwater Summit 2026 entered its second day in New York City, conversations moved forwards from where communications and marketing are headed, to how brands can actively shape what comes next.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Across sessions on AI-powered workflows, creator-led campaigns, corporate storytelling, crisis preparedness, and data-driven decision-making, speakers explored what it takes to build trust and relevance in an increasingly fragmented information landscape.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"From the FIFA World Cup’s cultural strategy to Mars’ efforts to connect its corporate brand to everyday consumers, Day 2 highlighted a communications industry balancing rapid technological change with a renewed focus on authenticity and human connection.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here’s a recap of the biggest insights and themes from Day 2 at Meltwater Summit 2026. (You can check out day 1 here.)","spans":[{"start":96,"end":116,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"afskAhIAACcAJOm5","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"key-takeaways-from-meltwater-summit-2026-day-1","first_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:52:15+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T12:37:32+0000","uid":"meltwater-summit-2026-day-1-key-takeaways","url":"/en/blog/meltwater-summit-2026-day-1-key-takeaways","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Inside the Marketing Playbook of the FIFA World Cup","spans":[{"start":0,"end":51,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How AI Is Reshaping Communications Strategy","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Brands Are Building Corporate Relevance Through Culture","spans":[{"start":0,"end":59,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Three Key Themes from Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQs About Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2","spans":[{"start":0,"end":38,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Inside the Marketing Playbook of the FIFA World Cup","spans":[{"start":0,"end":51,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Bettina Garibaldi, Chief Marketing \u0026 Communications Officer for the FIFA World Cup 2026 NYNJ Host Committee, opened Day 2 with an inside look at one of the world’s most ambitious marketing and operational undertakings.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The 2026 tournament will span three countries, 16 host cities, 48 qualifying teams, and 104 matches over 40 days, culminating with the final at MetLife Stadium in New Jersey. But beyond the scale, Garibaldi emphasized the importance of making the World Cup feel personal and culturally relevant to local audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Rather than treating the event purely as a sports moment, FIFA is positioning the World Cup as a broader cultural experience that intersects with fashion, food, creators, music, and community engagement. Fan zones, art installations, local ambassador programs, and neighborhood initiatives are all designed to connect with audiences far beyond core soccer fans.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Garibaldi also highlighted how AI is helping streamline workflows and improve operational efficiency while reinforcing the importance of preparedness, crisis planning, and cross-agency coordination for an event of this scale.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The real creativity is in identifying how I can reach somebody who might not be interested in soccer. My job is to ensure we are creating and designing moments and experiences that people want to be a part of. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":210,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Bettina Garibaldi, FIFA World Cup 2026 NYNJ Host Committee","spans":[{"start":0,"end":58,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How AI Is Reshaping Communications Strategy","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Artificial intelligence remained one of the defining conversations throughout Day 2, with speakers exploring how AI is transforming everything from audience targeting and content creation to crisis management and analytics workflows.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"During “The Smart Shift: Using AI to Supercharge Comms Strategy,” Meltwater’s Antony Cousins outlined how the industry has evolved from traditional media, to algorithm-driven social feeds, and now into an era of AI-powered personalization at scale.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Instead of communicating to audience segments, brands increasingly face a future of “infinite personalization,” where AI tools tailor information to individuals based on their interests, values, and behaviors. Cousins emphasized that while AI can automate processes and accelerate analysis, human strengths like empathy, judgment, creativity, and strategic thinking remain irreplaceable.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"He also described how AI agents and skills-based architectures are changing the way communicators interact with software, shifting teams away from manual workflows and toward more goal-oriented systems.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI can deliver the lightning bug all day long. Humans still need to bring the lightning. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":88,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Antony Cousins, Meltwater","spans":[{"start":0,"end":25,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That same balance between technology and human insight appeared in multiple sessions throughout the day. In “From Data to Decisions,” panelists from Havas PR, We Communications, Point600, Marketbridge, and Meltwater emphasized that AI is only as useful as the context and structure behind the data powering it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meanwhile, Noah Greenberg, CEO of Stacker, explored how AI-powered search is changing discoverability online. As large language models increasingly rely on earned media citations when generating responses, communications teams have an opportunity to shape visibility through trusted editorial coverage rather than traditional SEO alone.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Together, these sessions reinforced that rather than replacing communications professionals, AI is elevating the importance of strategic communicators who can provide context, direction, and trust.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"embed","oembed":{"embed_url":"https://www.linkedin.com/posts/meltwater_how-do-llms-surface-and-cite-content-from-activity-7457537780676632577-TLTV?utm_source=share\u0026utm_medium=member_desktop\u0026rcm=ACoAAAC2leYBxFVb_mFj4qWBYsz2g99XjTh0AS4","type":"link","version":"1.0","title":"How do LLMs surface and cite content from LinkedIn? \n\nAt Meltwater Summit, Chris Hackney from Meltwater and Gina Kleiner from LinkedIn for Marketing just took the stage to tease something big: a new… | Meltwater","provider_name":"article","thumbnail_url":"https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQGGOGWbKDbdsA/feedshare-shrink_800/B4EZ35_GB6IoAc-/0/1778015560305?e=2147483647\u0026v=beta\u0026t=nHgZcAjkMdMRyuz2UxX5b7axVGHKghb4MnxaAreNa3g","html":"\u003cdiv data-type=\"article\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/posts/meltwater_how-do-llms-surface-and-cite-content-from-activity-7457537780676632577-TLTV\"\u003e\u003ch1\u003eHow do LLMs surface and cite content from LinkedIn? \n\nAt Meltwater Summit, Chris Hackney from Meltwater and Gina Kleiner from LinkedIn for Marketing just took the stage to tease something big: a new… | Meltwater\u003c/h1\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQGGOGWbKDbdsA/feedshare-shrink_800/B4EZ35_GB6IoAc-/0/1778015560305?e=2147483647\u0026v=beta\u0026t=nHgZcAjkMdMRyuz2UxX5b7axVGHKghb4MnxaAreNa3g\"\u003e\u003cp\u003eHow do LLMs surface and cite content from LinkedIn? \n\nAt Meltwater Summit, Chris Hackney from Meltwater and Gina Kleiner from LinkedIn for Marketing just took the stage to tease something big: a new report that breaks down which types of LinkedIn content get surfaced and cited on LMMs.\n\nAnd the early findings might surprise you…\n\n✨ LinkedIn is the 2nd most-cited source across AI models\n✨ Over 70% of citations come from members with under 100K followers\n✨ Members with under 10K followers drive 35% of citations\n\nThe takeaway: If you want to show up in AI-generated answers, your LinkedIn strategy needs a rethink.\n\nThe full report by Meltwater drops soon… stay tuned.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e","url":"https://www.linkedin.com/posts/meltwater_how-do-llms-surface-and-cite-content-from-activity-7457537780676632577-TLTV"}},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Brands Are Building Corporate Relevance Through Culture","spans":[{"start":0,"end":59,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Several Day 2 sessions explored how brands are moving beyond traditional corporate messaging to create stronger emotional and cultural connections with audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ana Baptista from Mars Incorporated shared how the company uses communications to support business-critical priorities by increasing awareness of the broader Mars portfolio, from pet nutrition and veterinary health to sustainability and local economic impact.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Using data-backed audience insights, Mars discovered that the more consumers understood the full scope of the company’s work, the more positively they viewed the corporate brand. Campaigns like “I Was Today Years Old When I Learned” used short-form social storytelling to connect familiar consumer brands like M\u0026M’s and Pedigree back to Mars itself, particularly among Gen Z audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"At the same time, Mars paired emotional storytelling with measurable business objectives. Through partnerships with Calm and research into the human-animal bond, the company connected its pet care business to broader conversations around mental wellness and emotional health.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"To know Mars is to love Mars. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":29,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ana Baptista, Mars Incorporated","spans":[{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The emphasis on authenticity and emotional connection also appeared in sessions focused on creators and influencer partnerships.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"SHEIN’s Lisa Zlotnick and Jennifer Brown explained how the brand’s Festival House campaign generated hundreds of millions of impressions by integrating creators directly into the campaign experience and allowing them creative freedom to produce authentic content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Later in the day, a panel featuring creator Alaska, Canon’s Gareth Crew, and Meltwater’s Neil Brennan explored how platforms like Snapchat are reshaping creator marketing through more personal, high-frequency audience engagement.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Speakers emphasized that creators increasingly function less like paid amplifiers and more like community builders with highly engaged audiences. Rather than polished one-off sponsorships, successful partnerships often center around authentic behind-the-scenes storytelling and ongoing interaction.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Three Key Themes from Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Across sessions featuring experts from FIFA, YouTube, Mars, Microsoft, Canva, Six Flags, Havas PR, News Corp Australia, SHEIN, and more, three themes consistently emerged throughout the day.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Authentic communities are more valuable than mass reach.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":59,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Whether discussing YouTube creators, Snapchat audiences, or creator-led campaigns, speakers consistently emphasized that influence now comes from trust and community connection rather than scale alone.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Andrew Peterson from YouTube highlighted how niche communities have become some of the platform’s most powerful engagement drivers, with creators building long-term trust through personalized, highly specific content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Similarly, speakers from Canon and Snapchat discussed how creators can drive stronger engagement by integrating brands naturally into everyday content rather than relying on polished campaign messaging alone.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The consistent takeaway: audiences increasingly reward authenticity, consistency, and relatability over traditional advertising approaches.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. Trust is now an operational priority.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":40,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Trust and reputation management appeared throughout Day 2, particularly as AI-generated misinformation, narrative attacks, and deepfakes become more sophisticated.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"During “Trust Under Pressure,” executives from Microsoft, Blackbird.AI, and We Communications explored how communications teams must now distinguish between genuine public backlash and coordinated disinformation campaigns in real time.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"At the same time, McKenna Kelley from Jabil demonstrated how proactive crisis monitoring, localized listening strategies, and cross-functional stakeholder alignment can help brands identify issues before they escalate.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For many organizations, communications teams are increasingly becoming central players in organizational resilience and risk management.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Trust today is continuously computed, happening in almost real time. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":68,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Michael McLoughlin, Microsoft","spans":[{"start":0,"end":29,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Data only matters if people understand the story behind it.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":62,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"From PR measurement to executive presentations, multiple sessions reinforced that data becomes valuable only when it drives clear action and understanding.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Genevieve Brammall from News Corp Australia shared how her PR Value Score framework helped connect communications activity directly to business outcomes, moving PR conversations beyond vanity metrics and into measurable organizational impact.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meanwhile, Canva’s Duncan Clark explored how visualization and storytelling dramatically improve audience comprehension and engagement with data. Rather than overwhelming audiences with dashboards and spreadsheets, he encouraged communicators to focus on clarity, pacing, narrative, and memorable visuals.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"No one ever makes a decision because of a number. They need a story. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":69,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Duncan Clark, Canva \u0026 Flourish","spans":[{"start":0,"end":30,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As Day 2 of Meltwater Summit 2026 came to a close, the conversations reflected an industry in transition,  one where AI is accelerating workflows, creators are reshaping influence, and audiences are demanding greater trust and authenticity from brands.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But across every session, one idea remained constant: technology alone is not enough. The brands and communicators who succeed in this new environment will be the ones that combine data, creativity, empathy, and cultural understanding to create stories people genuinely care about.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs About Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2","spans":[{"start":0,"end":38,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. What were the biggest themes at Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 2?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":63,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Day 2 focused heavily on AI-powered communications, creator-led storytelling, trust and reputation management, and the growing importance of authentic audience engagement.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. How is AI changing communications workflows?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Speakers discussed how AI is helping teams automate repetitive tasks, analyze larger volumes of data, personalize content, and improve operational efficiency. However, many also stressed that human creativity, empathy, and strategic judgment remain essential.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. What did speakers say about creator marketing?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sessions from YouTube, SHEIN, Canon, and Snapchat highlighted how creators are evolving from campaign amplifiers into long-term community builders and trusted voices with highly engaged audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Why is earned media becoming more important in the AI era?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":61,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As AI-powered search engines increasingly rely on trusted editorial citations when generating responses, earned media is becoming a major driver of AI visibility and discoverability online.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. How are brands connecting corporate reputation to culture?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":61,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Companies like Mars emphasized the importance of helping audiences understand the broader story behind the brands they already know and trust, using data-backed storytelling and cultural relevance to strengthen corporate These have real financial impacts, so actively detecting misinformation is an absolute must. Otherwise, you end up reacting to damage late instead of preventing it or nipping it in the bud.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What Is Misinformation Detection (And Why It’s Harder Than It Sounds)","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Misinformation detection is finding and flagging false or misleading content.","spans":[{"start":28,"end":76,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"At scale, though, it gets complicated.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Along with dealing with fake news, you’re sorting through misleading statistics, out-of-context claims, manipulated images and videos, and coordinated narratives designed to look credible.","spans":[{"start":24,"end":33,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YKepIRIAACIADSTM","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu","blog"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"fighting-fake-news-on-social-media","first_publication_date":"2021-07-08T06:49:44+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-03-24T23:26:03+0000","uid":"fake-news-on-social-media","url":"/en/blog/fake-news-on-social-media","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"All of it spreads across multiple channels at the same time, which makes it harder to track and even harder to contain.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That’s why modern tools lean on a mix of technologies. They use machine learning to spot patterns and language processing to understand context and tone. Visual analysis can detect altered media, while network tracking can see how information moves and who’s amplifying it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Because misinformation can take multiple forms and go viral in an instant, manual monitoring just can’t keep up. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Top Misinformation Detection Tools 2026 You Should Know","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"There’s no shortage of tools claiming to solve misinformation. Most of them sound similar on the surface, but they don’t all solve the same problem.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Some focus on real-time monitoring. Others go deeper into verification and analysis. A few can help you avoid unreliable sources altogether.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The key is knowing what you actually need, whether that’s early detection, deeper investigation, or better visibility into how narratives are spreading.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here are the platforms worth paying attention to right now:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":59,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Meltwater","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Logically AI","spans":[{"start":0,"end":12,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"NewsGuard","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Blackbird.AI","spans":[{"start":0,"end":12,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Brandwatch","spans":[{"start":0,"end":10,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Meltwater","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater acts as your early warning system. By the time a spike shows up in a weekly report, it’s already a problem.","spans":[{"start":31,"end":36,"type":"em"},{"start":56,"end":62,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater flips that. It compresses the gap between signal and action so your team can move while there’s still time to shape the narrative, not just react to it. That’s a huge difference when conversations can spiral in hours.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It also centralizes what’s usually scattered. Instead of jumping between tools to piece together what’s happening, you get one clear view across channels. That kind of visibility makes alignment easier across PR, social, comms, and leadership teams. Everyone sees the same story, which means faster decisions and fewer mixed messages when it matters most.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Interested in learning more? Logically AI digs into why it’s trending and who is pushing it. That’s critical when misinformation isn’t random but coordinated. If you’re dealing with organized campaigns, surface-level insights won’t cut it.","spans":[{"start":97,"end":100,"type":"em"},{"start":119,"end":122,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The human + AI approach matters more than most teams expect. Instead of reacting to questionable content after it spreads, you filter brand risk at the source. That’s a smarter way to reduce noise, especially when your team is overwhelmed with volume. If you know which outlets consistently publish unreliable information, you can deprioritize them before they influence your strategy.","spans":[{"start":106,"end":116,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ad1jlhEAAC4Ae5sJ","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"brand-risk-management-strategies-to-protect-brand-value","first_publication_date":"2026-04-14T21:59:18+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-01T05:27:19+0000","uid":"brand-risk-management","url":"/en/blog/brand-risk-management","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s also a strong fit for teams focused on long-term credibility. It detects, analyzes, and predicts how information operations and disinformation campaigns evolve across digital ecosystems.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":12,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://blackbird.ai/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeejfsBOoF08xL1M_Blackbird.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Blackbird AI homepage","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1600,"height":727},"id":"aeejfsBOoF08xL1M","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"What it does well:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Identifies narrative manipulation in real time","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Maps how stories move across platforms and communities","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Uses AI to predict potential reputational threats","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Best for: brands facing complex narrative risk or operating in high-stakes environments","spans":[{"start":0,"end":10,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This is where things move beyond monitoring into foresight. Start with this:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Identify where your risk actually lives","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Before you spend money on a tool, take a hard look at where misinformation is most likely to affect your brand. That could be social media, news coverage, internal comms, or a mix of all three.","spans":[{"start":155,"end":169,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YgDNzhIAACoAW1zm","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"internal-communications-best-practices-to-drive-your-business-forward","first_publication_date":"2022-02-07T09:14:51+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T14:03:04+0000","uid":"internal-communications","url":"/en/blog/internal-communications","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The point is to get specific early. When teams skip this step, they usually end up paying for more than they need.","spans":[{"start":16,"end":34,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Focus on the features that will actually help your team","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A long feature list can look impressive. But that doesn’t mean your team will use half of it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Keep your attention on the capabilities that make your response faster and easier to manage. Real-time monitoring and strong AI and NLP matter in brand reputation management.","spans":[{"start":118,"end":142,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abRYrREAACsALQ5z","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-in-brand-reputation-management-an-overview","first_publication_date":"2026-03-13T22:28:41+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-15T07:39:39+0000","uid":"ai-brand-reputation-management","url":"/en/blog/ai-brand-reputation-management","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":146,"end":173,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XmjPQxIAACIAfMRg","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu","pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"business-reputation-management-how-to-create-and-maintain-a-good-reputation","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T16:14:03+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T10:40:43+0000","uid":"how-a-business-can-create-and-maintain-a-good-reputation","url":"/en/blog/how-a-business-can-create-and-maintain-a-good-reputation","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Dashboards matter: if your team can’t make sense of the data quickly, the tool won’t help much. Custom alerts are also worth prioritizing, so you can catch important shifts without drowning in noise.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aN1zrREAAC4AZDX6","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"unified-dashboards-now-available-for-meltwater-customers","first_publication_date":"2025-10-02T14:35:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-10-02T14:39:07+0000","uid":"unified-dashboards-launch-announcement","url":"/en/blog/unified-dashboards-launch-announcement","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Scalability is also important: the tool you choose should still make sense a year from now, especially if your volume grows or your needs change.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Pay attention to integrations","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Integrations can make or break adoption. When a tool doesn’t connect to the systems your team already uses, it usually ends up collecting dust.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Your detection platform should work smoothly with your CRM and any other internal workflows. That way, information keeps moving and helps your team act faster when something starts to escalate.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"During a crisis, extra friction is a problem all by itself. The fewer workarounds your team needs, the better.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":110,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Real Payoff: Why Misinformation Detection Software Actually Matters","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Things move quickly once misinformation starts circulating. Having visibility early gives your team a chance to step in with context and a clear response before it escalates.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Protect your brand before it takes a hit","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Misinformation spreads and starts shaping perception before most teams even realize it’s happening.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Detection tools give you a head start. You can spot issues early and step in with clarity, guiding the conversation before it picks up momentum.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Keep your data (and decisions) on track","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The quality of your decision-making is driven by the quality of your data. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Clean, accurate inputs make it easier to respond and adjust in real time. Your strategy holds up better, and so does your execution.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Strengthen trust over time","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Trust builds through how you show up, especially when things get messy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When you catch misinformation early and address it directly, people notice. It signals that you’re paying attention and willing to step in when it matters. Over time, that consistency adds up, and it’s something a lot of brands miss.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Stay In Control with Meltwater ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Misinformation moves fast, and it’s not getting any easier to keep up. Once it starts circulating, it doesn’t take long for it to shape how people see your brand.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If you’re not tracking it as it happens, you’re stepping in after the narrative has already taken hold.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aDo8WSdWJ-7kSwg8_Brand-Analytics-1-.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"An example of AI powered insights within the Meltwater social listening tool, Explore","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":2250,"height":1405},"id":"aDo8WSdWJ-7kSwg8","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater gives you a clear view of what’s gaining attention, why it’s picking up momentum, and where it’s spreading. That visibility makes it easier to step in early, respond with context, and keep things from escalating further.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Learn when you request a demo by filling out the form below.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":60,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Scroll-To-Form"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs About Misinformation Detection Software","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What types of misinformation can these tools detect?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Misinformation detection tools can detect a wide range of misinformation, including: Fake news, deepfakes, misleading stats, out-of-context content, and coordinated campaigns. If it spreads digitally, these tools are built to catch it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How accurate are AI-driven systems?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI-driven misinformation detection systems are very accurate, and they keep getting better. While no system is 100% infallible, advanced AI models leverage vast datasets and complex algorithms to identify patterns and anomalies with precision and speed, significantly improving more manual methods. Most platforms combine AI with human oversight, which serves to maintain accuracy while keeping speed high. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Do teams need training to use misinformation detection tools?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Short answer: yes. Long answer: not much, but some training is necessary for teams to actually be able to use advanced features like set up effective alerts, generate useful reports, and use advanced features like dashboards.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Can they tell the difference between satire and misinformation?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sometimes. Advanced NLP helps, but some systems still trip up over recognizing satire vs misinformation. That’s where human review remains essential for accurate detection.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"afskAhIAACcAJOm5","uid":"meltwater-summit-2026-day-1-key-takeaways","url":"/en/blog/meltwater-summit-2026-day-1-key-takeaways","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22afskAhIAACcAJOm5%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:52:15+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T12:37:32+0000","slugs":["key-takeaways-from-meltwater-summit-2026-day-1"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"Key Takeaways from Meltwater Summit 2026: Day 1","published_date":"2026-05-06","author_name":"Ann-Derrick Gaillot","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Marketing"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afsxN8BOoF08xq7M_BlogImage.png?auto=format,compress","id":"afsxN8BOoF08xq7M","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afsxN8BOoF08xq7M_BlogImage.png?auto=format%2Ccompress\u0026rect=204%2C0%2C1280%2C1280\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afsxN8BOoF08xq7M","edit":{"x":204,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Times Square was buzzing more than usual as Meltwater Summit 2026 kicked off, bringing together PR, marketing, and communications leaders for a day of collective insight and inspiration. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Major product announcements and candid conversations with industry leaders gave attendees an exclusive look at how the worlds of marketing, PR, and communications look now and where they are headed next. Here’s a brief recap of the excitement and industry knowledge that Summit brought to the heart of New York City this year.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Meltwater’s New AI-Powered Innovations for Modern Comms Teams","spans":[{"start":0,"end":61,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Gwyneth Paltrow on Building a Brand Rooted in Authenticity","spans":[{"start":0,"end":58,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Three Key Themes from Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQs About Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1","spans":[{"start":0,"end":38,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Meltwater’s New AI-Powered Innovations for Modern Comms Teams","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Summit 2026 began with an exciting look at some of the latest Meltwater product innovations, introduced by CEO John Box. Following a live demonstration of GenAI Lens and an overview of Explore’s enhanced image and video analysis capabilities, Chief Product Officer Chris Hackney and Chief Technology Officer Aditya Jami announced the Meltwater Trend Center, the platform’s new hub for detecting emerging narratives across social media networks and news. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Discovery is the new monitoring, and we're focused on putting that power in your hands every single day.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":104,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Chris Hackney, Chief Product Officer, Meltwater","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In the age of AI-powered workflows, tools like the Trend Center and the new Mira integration with Slack let PR and communications professionals identify key narratives around their brands in minutes instead of hours or days. Together, these and the other new innovations included in Meltwater’s 2026 Mid-Year Release highlighted how faster, smarter workflows empower teams to anticipate and actually shape the narratives that matter to them most.","spans":[{"start":283,"end":316,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/about/press-releases/2026-mid-year-release","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Gwyneth Paltrow on Building a Brand Rooted in Authenticity","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In her keynote conversation with Meltwater Chief Strategy Officer Alexandra Bjertnaes, Paltrow emphasized how important brand storytelling and clear values have been to Goop’s success. She described how, instead of just creating products or pursuing quick growth, Goop prioritized integrity, giving the brand room to experiment and build audience trust in the long term. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ciframe src=\"https://www.linkedin.com/embed/feed/update/urn:li:share:7457512254318571520\" height=\"941\" width=\"504\" frameborder=\"0\" allowfullscreen=\"\" title=\"Embedded post\"\u003e\u003c/iframe\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Along the process of building Goop, Paltrow learned how to find her “zone of excellence” and effectively delegate what falls outside of it. And while she acknowledged the importance of data, Paltrow stressed that intuition has been a key part of moving the brand forward.  ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Know the values of the brand specifically. Know your unique value. Why do the people that love you love you? Be as authentic to that as possible.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":145,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Gwyneth Paltrow","spans":[{"start":0,"end":15,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Overall, Paltrow’s candid conversation showed the audience the power of authenticity in action, reminding just how much of an impact staying true to your brand’s voice can have.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Three Key Themes from Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Across 20 sessions featuring experts from NBCUniversal, McDonald’s, Novartis, The Associated Press, and many more, attendees learned how to tackle today’s most pressing PR and marketing challenges. Here are three of the many key takeaways that emerged over the course of the day.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Partner with storytellers to drive impact.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Several speakers echoed Gwyneth Paltrow in their emphasis on authentic, people-centered storytelling for building trust and having tangible business impact. As Lonely Planet’s Deepa Lakshmin and Shalayne Pulia explained during their session, intentional, ongoing collaboration with their network of creators was key to achieving benchmark social media engagement and conversion rates for the brand. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For Cisco’s Paul Wendel, establishing partnerships with trusted storytellers proved to be the best avenue for expanding brand reach and driving trust. And similarly, Hilary Smith of NBCUniversal explained how its Creative Impact Lab forges partnerships between employees, nonprofits, storytellers, and brands to achieve its corporate social responsibility goals. In 2025, public service announcements created by the Lab generated 550 million impressions and $28 million in donated commercial time.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It is so important to forge partnerships in corporate social responsibility. We cannot move the needle alone. In CSR, one plus one equals three. If I'm going to make an impact, I need others to join in with me.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":210,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Hilary Smith, Executive Vice President of Corporate Social Responsibility, NBCUniversal","spans":[{"start":0,"end":87,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. Use strategic data to thrive in the AI era. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In her conversation with Sarah Elizabeth Graham from Patreon, Sara Myles from McDonald's shared how connecting communications data to real outcomes helps teams drive meaningful impact. She and her team built AI agents for corporate voice, audience, and intelligence to inform their planning and strategy development. Michael Capone explained how his Corporate Affairs Intelligence team at Novartis has integrated AI into every step of their processes, freeing up more time for strategic thinking and planning. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"And speaking from a different perspective, neuroscientist Carmen Simon of Enhancive shared her research findings showing that AI content captures attention but does not always lead to long-term memory. As a result, she explained, brands need to challenge themselves to make qualities like human oversight and emotional depth visible as they strive to build trust with their audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ultimately, we all have the tools now. We all have the data. Now, the only thing that you need to do to change and start affecting the enterprise at the highest level with respect to social intelligence is change the way you ask questions of the data.\nMichael Capone, Director of Integrated Insights \u0026 Research, Novartis","spans":[{"start":0,"end":251,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}},{"start":252,"end":320,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Understand context to find clarity in the noise. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Across multiple sessions, speakers emphasized how dependent brand success is on the ability to separate meaningful signals from noise and act on them. Meltwater’s Jenny Force and G2’s Palmer Hutchins explained how LLMs are reshaping discovery, making contextual validation across sources a new foundation for brand visibility.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In conversation with Orchestra’s Jonathan Rosen, Dan Brahmy of Cyabra highlighted how bots and content overload distort reality. In response, brands need to prioritize understanding emerging narratives before they react. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meanwhile, McDonald’s Amanda Mulligan and Wieden+Kennedy’s Russell Pinke discussed how using social listening to surface “fan truths”, i.e. shared rituals and behaviors among fans, helps them create viral campaigns. Together, these experts drove home that data is just the first step in truly understanding audiences. Interpreting and contextualizing that data bridges the gap between interesting insights and impactful action.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Be curious, and look for insights and not trends. Look for things that truly speak to the core behaviors of your audience, and pocket those insights.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":149,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Amanda Mulligan, Director of Social Media and Creators, McDonald’s","spans":[{"start":0,"end":66,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The conversations, presentations, and workshops of Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1 laid out how clarity and intentionality are as valuable as ever in today’s communications and marketing landscape. As the industry changes keep coming, brands that remain curious, critical, and consistent will stay ahead, winning their audience’s attention and trust.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Understand the forces shaping your brand and use them to your advantage. See how it works in Meltwater’s Mid-Year 2026 Product Release.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":135,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":93,"end":134,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/product-updates-mid-year-2026","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs About Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. What were the biggest announcements at Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":70,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Day 1 featured several major product announcements, including the launch of the Meltwater Trend Center, new AI-powered workflows, expanded image and video analysis capabilities in Explore, and a new Mira integration with Slack designed to help communications teams identify emerging narratives faster.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. What did Gwyneth Paltrow discuss during her keynote?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":55,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Gwyneth Paltrow spoke about building Goop through authentic storytelling, clear brand values, and long-term audience trust. She also emphasized the importance of intuition, delegation, and staying true to a brand’s unique identity.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. What themes emerged across Summit Day 1 sessions?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":52,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Three major themes stood out across the day’s sessions: the importance of authentic storytelling partnerships, the growing role of strategic data and AI in communications, and the need to understand context in order to separate meaningful insights from noise.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. How are AI tools changing PR and communications workflows?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":61,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Speakers highlighted how AI is helping teams automate repetitive tasks, surface emerging narratives more quickly, and connect communications data to business outcomes. Many also stressed that human oversight and contextual understanding remain essential for building trust and creating memorable content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. Who spoke at Meltwater Summit 2026 Day 1?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":44,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Day 1 featured speakers and experts from organizations including NBCUniversal, McDonald’s, Novartis, Lonely Planet, Patreon, Cisco, The Associated Press, G2, Cyabra, and more, alongside keynote speaker Gwyneth Paltrow.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"afijjBIAACwAIRn2","uid":"substack-brand-marketing-examples","url":"/en/blog/substack-brand-marketing-examples","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22afijjBIAACwAIRn2%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2026-05-06T12:57:05+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T13:51:38+0000","slugs":["what-is-substack-marketing-how-leading-brands-use-it"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"What Is Substack Marketing? How Leading Brands Use It","published_date":"2026-05-04","author_name":"Ann-Derrick Gaillot","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":null,"categories":[{"category1":"Marketing"},{"category1":"Content Marketing"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"The substack logo against an orange stippled background","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afs5oMBOoF08xrBm_Substack_1920x1200copy3.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=60,0,1800,1200\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"afs5oMBOoF08xrBm","edit":{"x":60,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":"The substack logo against an orange stippled background","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afs5oMBOoF08xrBm_Substack_1920x1200copy3.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=360,0,1200,1200\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afs5oMBOoF08xrBm","edit":{"x":360,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Substack was once just a niche platform for independent writers, but in recent years it has emerged as a valuable channel in today's marketing ecosystem. Major brands like Walmart are sponsoring newsletters on the platform, while others like Shopify are launching their own altogether. What they’re seeking is the brand awareness and relationship building opportunities that come along with the direct, personality-driven content newsletters can uniquely offer. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If you’re reading this, you’re probably wondering how big of an opportunity Substack is for your brand or how others are actually leveraging the platform in practice. Read on to learn exactly how leading brands are making the most of this promising channel and what that means for you. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Social media mentions of Substack have increased by about 30% over the past six months. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":88,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}},{"start":0,"end":88,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How popular is Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater Explore data shows that Substack is a hot topic, generating over 5 million social media mentions from November 1, 2025, to April 29, 2026. That’s a nearly 30% increase compared to the six months prior.","spans":[{"start":75,"end":107,"type":"strong"},{"start":165,"end":177,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ciframe width=\"871\" height=\"560\" seamless frameborder=\"0\" scrolling=\"no\" src=\"https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/e/2PACX-1vSkOJeg9yykF7jf8ttIuE-uGKiT01n5_N1V5qAtBZMG5W6BA_Sr7AT5CLfG9_VnuEgeKMYg9bipIEnz/pubchart?oid=3586284\u0026amp;format=interactive\"\u003e\u003c/iframe\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"About 46% of those mentions came from Bluesky, with another 38% coming from X. Both text-based platforms lend themselves well to writers and brands promoting their own newsletters, as well as the kind of back-and-forth discussion that long-form, written content can inspire. ","spans":[{"start":38,"end":45,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Z2rc9REAACcAtBkf","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-bluesky-discover-social-medias-latest-star","first_publication_date":"2024-12-24T16:19:13+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-04-23T18:43:16+0000","uid":"what-is-bluesky","url":"/en/blog/what-is-bluesky","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"However, despite smaller shares of voice, even the visual-first platforms of YouTube, Instagram, and Pinterest generate notable volumes of Substack mentions. Collectively, those three platforms racked up over 457,000 in the six-month period analyzed. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afikNsBOoF08xlA3_Screenshot2026-04-29at11.57.27AM-Edited.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Screenshot of an Instagram post by @charli_xcx promoting an essay titled “The Death of Cool,” with a caption mentioning it accompanies the cover of Arabbit’s Foot by Hailey Benton Gates and is available on Substack; engagement metrics are visible below.","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":624,"height":370},"id":"afikNsBOoF08xlA3","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This screenshot from the Meltwater platform shows the caption of Charli XCX’s now-deleted Instagram post promoting her Substack page. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":134,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Image-Text"}},{"start":25,"end":43,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/platform","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In fact, Instagram was the source of the post that drove the most engagement overall. Charli XCX promoted her Substack essay examining the concept of “cool” and commodification in a now-deleted Instagram post that generated over 443,000 likes and 1,700 comments, likely inspiring a slew of new subscriptions to “charli’s substack” in the process.","spans":[{"start":106,"end":124,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://itscharlibb.substack.com/p/the-death-of-cool","target":"_blank"}},{"start":182,"end":193,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.dazeddigital.com/life-culture/article/70150/1/why-is-everyone-wiping-their-instagram-grids-charli-xcx-bad-bunny","target":"_blank"}},{"start":311,"end":330,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://itscharlibb.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Do Brands Use Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Today, brands are primarily using Substack for marketing in three ways:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Sponsorships: These are essentially influencer partnerships, with brands letting writers take the content creation lead in introducing them to audiences.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"},{"start":36,"end":59,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZbpNgRIAAC4ABxNW","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-management-how-to-build-successful-relationships","first_publication_date":"2024-02-23T16:46:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-02T12:29:31+0000","uid":"influencer-management","url":"/en/blog/influencer-management","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Branded Newsletters: These are editorial operations providing regular, valuable content, often across different verticals. The heaviest lift option, they nevertheless offer an opportunity to cultivate an engaged community around your brand.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":21,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Founder and CEO Newsletters: These are the personal projects of brand leaders with something to say. They can be unpolished and wide-ranging, super niche, brand-focused, or even only tangentially related to the brand. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":29,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Each one offers a different balance of investment and audience ownership for brands looking to tap into Substack’s ecosystem.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Sponsorships","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Many brands reach Substack audiences via partnerships with existing newsletters and writers. This kind of setup isn’t exactly new. As we covered in our previous roundup of brands on Substack, several companies have had established newsletter sponsorships on the platform at least as far back as 2024. However, with the platform’s new native sponsorship program and continued user growth we may see even more organizations adopting this model. ","spans":[{"start":161,"end":190,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Z_kv8REAACkAnaEg","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-brands-are-leveraging-the-substack-boom","first_publication_date":"2025-04-11T15:13:16+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:21:55+0000","uid":"brands-on-substack","url":"/en/blog/brands-on-substack","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":271,"end":299,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.modernretail.co/marketing/were-all-just-fumbling-around-advertising-on-substack-remains-a-wild-west-for-brands/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":315,"end":360,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://substack.com/@hamish/note/c-185976913","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As with all content creator partnerships, the key to success here is brand fit. Luckily for brands, there are 50,000 monetized Substack newsletters to choose from, with five million paying subscribers and tens of millions more reading for free. ","spans":[{"start":110,"end":147,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.nytimes.com/2025/05/10/business/substack-newsletter-subscription-costs.html","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands are linking up with the platform’s most successful writers and communities to promote specific initiatives and campaigns, as well as boost general visibility. Take for example:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Walmart inviting a popular daily business newsletter cover its small business initiative event, Open Call ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":7,"type":"strong"},{"start":19,"end":52,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.readfeedme.com/p/most-of-you-guessed-why-i-was-in","target":"_blank"}},{"start":53,"end":88,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Banana Republic featuring a dedicated section highlighting its Friends and Family sale within an issue of a lifestyle newsletter","spans":[{"start":0,"end":15,"type":"strong"},{"start":46,"end":86,"type":"strong"},{"start":97,"end":128,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://joannagoddard.substack.com/p/dating-sense-of-humor","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Hinge providing data from its Gen-Z dating report for a special edition of a popular personal newsletter","spans":[{"start":0,"end":5,"type":"strong"},{"start":15,"end":49,"type":"strong"},{"start":64,"end":104,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://eviegoodman.substack.com/p/i-went-on-a-first-date-with-a-male","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Lenovo having its Yoga Tab Plus AI tablet mentioned in this essay about writing in a digital culture newsletter","spans":[{"start":0,"end":6,"type":"strong"},{"start":18,"end":34,"type":"strong"},{"start":35,"end":51,"type":"strong"},{"start":60,"end":79,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://madisonhuizinga.substack.com/p/there-is-no-logging-off","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Sutter Home having its wines included in a short video within this recipe post on a cooking newsletter","spans":[{"start":0,"end":12,"type":"strong"},{"start":23,"end":42,"type":"strong"},{"start":43,"end":54,"type":"strong"},{"start":62,"end":78,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://justaddhotsauce.substack.com/p/chickpea-and-black-eyed-pea-salad","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Brooklinen helping a fashion and culture newsletter to do a subscription giveaway","spans":[{"start":0,"end":10,"type":"strong"},{"start":19,"end":51,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.themolehill.net/p/four-outfits-inspired-by-princess","target":"_blank"}},{"start":60,"end":81,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"What makes the best brand sponsorships work is seamless integration. Audiences are there to follow the writers, so brands should blend into the experience or enhance it with perks like free access to newsletters that are normally gated. Overall, the sponsorship route is a lower lift than dedicated newsletters that gives brands the opportunity to work with multiple writers reaching different niche audiences.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Branded Newsletters","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Fashion and beauty brands like Rare Beauty, Madewell, and Shopify are creating Substacks that are full-on digital magazines. As such, they feature mixes of content types, like interviews, personal essays, product spotlights, and behind-the-scenes features, that can maintain readers’ attention over time and build trust along the way.","spans":[{"start":31,"end":42,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://rarebeauty.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":44,"end":52,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://wellsaidbymadewell.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":58,"end":65,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://shopify.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":176,"end":186,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://rarebeauty.substack.com/p/behind-the-marketing-strategy","target":"_blank"}},{"start":188,"end":203,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://wellsaidbymadewell.substack.com/p/growing-up-with-alexa-chung","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Though branded newsletters require much more resource investment than sponsorships, they also offer more possibilities for brand- and community-building in the long term.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afilC8BOoF08xlBu_Screenshot2026-04-30at10.59.40AM-Edited.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Homepage of “Rare Beauty Secrets” Substack with a large central banner reading “No, You’re Not Fine,” surrounded by articles about beauty and mental health; product images and editorial thumbnails appear alongside navigation tabs and a subscribe button.","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":3554,"height":1778},"id":"afilC8BOoF08xlBu","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Rare Beauty’s Substack page includes Mental Health as a main content pillar alongside Community and Beauty.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":107,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Image-Text"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For example, Rare Beauty’s Substack naturally covers beauty and the company’s inner workings, but it also allows the brand to highlight mental health as a pillar of its identity. Articles about wellness and self-care are regular offerings, featuring guest writers working directly in the mental health field. As a result, Rare Beauty is able to regularly share resources with its fans and followers, supplementing the work it does through its associated impact fund focused on youth mental health services and education.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meanwhile, Shopify’s Substack is geared for its audience of creators and entrepreneurs. Its newsletter issues highlight the small business using Shopify to build successful brands, like this one interviewing the founders of underwear brand Manmade. Through its celebration of its actual users through profiles and data-driven stories behind industry trends, Shopify gives readers valuable information that they can use in their own entrepreneurial journeys. The newsletter allows the brand to position itself as a small business resource and ally instead of just an e-commerce platform.","spans":[{"start":181,"end":194,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://shopify.substack.com/p/off-the-record-manmade","target":"_blank"}},{"start":314,"end":356,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://shopify.substack.com/p/tcg-boom","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Though launching and maintaining a dedicated brand newsletter is no small task, it creates more sustained opportunities for community building and engagement than sponsorships alone.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Founder and CEO Newsletters","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Some brands are driving exposure on Substack via their leaders’ newsletters. Less editorial and more bloggy, these personal dispatches offer readers the kind of candid perspectives and musings that serve to humanize the associated brand.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afilRsBOoF08xlB__d918260e-3837-4e80-ba57-3ec21ffed63d.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Homepage of a Substack newsletter titled “Night Shade,” featuring a grid of lifestyle and fashion posts with images of clothing, accessories, and home decor; a central article titled “A Book Tour and Some Mom Things” is highlighted, with a sidebar listing most popular posts.","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":3564,"height":1682},"id":"afilRsBOoF08xlB_","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The Night Shade newsletter covers everything from shopping hauls to gift guides to styling tips, as well as the occasional mention of the author’s company Ghia.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":160,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Image-Text"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"One great example is Night Shade by Melanie Masarin, the founder and CEO of non-alcoholic apéritifs brand Ghia. The newsletter boasts over 13,000 subscribers, but not every post mentions the brand. In fact, many don’t, making the newsletter feel authentic instead of just another marketing channel. By bringing readers into her everyday through issues about birthday celebrations, styling tips, and the occasional peek into the inner workings of the company, Melanie successfully casts Ghia as more than just a brand, but an aspect of a mindful, joyful lifestyle.","spans":[{"start":21,"end":32,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://substack.com/@melaniemasarin","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For more inspiration, check out: ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"A Little Bit of Everything by Rebecca Minkoff, founder of the eponymous fashion brand","spans":[{"start":0,"end":26,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://rebeccaminkoff.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"somstack by Alex Mill co-founder and creative director Somsack Sikhounmuong","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://somsacksikhounmuong.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"La Vie de Clare V. by Clare V. founder Clare Vivier","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.clarev.com/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Take Your Time by Crown Affair founder Dianna Cohen","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://dianna.substack.com/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If your brand has a founder or CEO who loves to write and share their perspectives with the world, a Substack may be a great option to highlight them.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Do Brands Find Substack Newsletters to Sponsor?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Many brands use similar strategies for finding newsletters to partner with as they do for finding influencers. And in some cases, newsletter discovery and influencer discovery can be accomplished using the same tools. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For Meltwater influencer marketing users, the AI Discovery feature lets you streamline the scouting process based on your brand identity and campaign goals. Just type in what you are looking for, or even copy and paste a creative brief, and the tool will direct you to the best writers to help you reach your campaign goals.","spans":[{"start":4,"end":34,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not a Meltwater user yet? Book a meeting today.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Should Your Brand Join Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It depends. Substack’s audience is growing, but it is still a niche platform that not every brand or marketing team has the resources to maintain. For many brands interested in the platform, newsletter partnerships are the best route, allowing you to reach an existing audience without having to build one from scratch.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"\n","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"However, brands that are looking to experiment with dedicated Substacks or want to commit to the long haul, here are three essential questions to ask from the start.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. What are your goals?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Looking to drive awareness? Build community? Support product launches? Publicize thought leadership? Whatever your objective is, clarifying it from the start will help you determine your approach and avoid time-consuming dead ends down the line.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. What is the focus of your newsletter and your brand’s point of view?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Successful Substacks have a clear niche, perspective, and voice. Think about what your brand can offer. Is it expertise and exclusive insights? Storytelling and cultural commentary? And how does that all align with your broader brand identity? A clear niche and point of view is a launchpad for success, you can’t start without them!","spans":[{"start":144,"end":156,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YSj6vREAACQAPRMJ","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu","blog"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"brand-storytelling-what-it-is-and-why-its-important","first_publication_date":"2021-11-06T14:06:45+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T11:09:19+0000","uid":"brand-story-storytelling","url":"/en/blog/brand-story-storytelling","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. What is your team or department’s capacity?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Creating a consistent, high-quality newsletter requires time, editorial direction, and continuous production. Make sure you have the resources to maintain a regular publishing cadence, or consider starting with partnerships before scaling into owned content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Final Thoughts","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The rise of Substack is part of a bigger shift in marketing where audiences are seeking out voices they can trust and going directly to them for their content and perspectives. Platforms like Substack are hubs for that shift in influence, where writers, creators, and brand leaders are able to directly access highly engaged audiences united by specific interests. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Substack isn’t a replacement for current marketing strategies and channels, but it’s an attractive venue for brands seeking to build influence with intentional audiences over time. The goal isn’t viral surges of clicks, but rather relationship building in an owned community.","spans":[{"start":33,"end":61,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XsJrIxEAAIuv77jy","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","site"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-an-effective-content-marketing-strategy-content-calendar-template","first_publication_date":"2020-05-18T11:07:06+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-09T11:19:47+0000","uid":"keys-to-effective-content-marketing-strategy","url":"/en/blog/keys-to-effective-content-marketing-strategy","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"\nThe newsletter route isn’t for every brand, but for those with a solid brand identity and voice, it’s worth a closer look.","spans":[{"start":72,"end":86,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Xmj8bBIAACIAfZiF","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"designing-and-refining-your-brand-identity","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T16:15:16+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:51:14+0000","uid":"how-to-design-a-new-brand-identity-for-your-business","url":"/en/blog/how-to-design-a-new-brand-identity-for-your-business","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Frequently Asked Questions: Brands on Substack","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is Substack and why is it growing?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Substack is a newsletter publishing platform that allows writers, creators, and brands to distribute content directly to subscribers. Its growth is driven by demand for independent media and direct audience relationships.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How popular is Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Substack is gaining traction, according to Meltwater data, with over 5 million social media mentions from Nov 1, 2025 to Apr 29, 2026, representing a 30% increase compared to the six months prior. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How are brands using Substack for marketing?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands typically use Substack in three ways:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Sponsorships","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Branded newsletters","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"o-list-item","text":"Founder-led newsletters","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Each one offers different balances of investment and audience ownership for brands looking to tap into Substack’s ecosystem.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Should every brand launch a Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not necessarily. Substack works best for brands with a clear voice and consistent content strategy. Additionally, dedicated newsletters require investment in continuous content creation.
