For many brands, newsletter sponsorship is the more feasible route. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do brands find Substack newsletters to partner with?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands use influencer discovery and audience analysis tools to identify relevant newsletters. Meltwater’s AI Discovery feature streamlines this process by matching brands with influencers with Substack newsletters based on audience fit, content themes, and campaign goals.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What makes a successful Substack partnership?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Successful partnerships feel native to the writer’s voice and provide real value to readers.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is the biggest opportunity for brands on Substack?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The biggest opportunity is building long-term, trust-based relationships with audiences. With Meltwater, brands can track how those relationships translate into engagement, sentiment, and overall brand impact.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"afkpLRIAACsAIee0","uid":"ai-misinformation-detect-harmful-narratives","url":"/en/blog/ai-misinformation-detect-harmful-narratives","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22afkpLRIAACsAIee0%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2026-05-05T00:07:35+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T07:20:07+0000","slugs":["ai-generated-misinformation-detect-harmful-narratives-in-llms"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"AI-Generated Misinformation: Detect Harmful Narratives in LLMs","published_date":"2026-05-04","author_name":"Chris Hanson","subtitle_tagline":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Track your presence in LLMs with GenAI Lens","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/ai-visibility-tracking","target":"_blank"}},{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"label","data":{"label":"cta-button"}}],"direction":"ltr"}],"category":null,"categories":[{"category1":"Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)"},{"category1":"Reputation Management"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"An image of two orange triangles on a reflective orange surface. AI-Generated Misinformation blog post. How to detect Harmful Narratives in LLMs","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afk0Q8BOoF08xmlL_AI-GeneratedMisinformation-DetectHarmfulNarrativesinLLMs_1920x1200.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=60,0,1800,1200\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"afk0Q8BOoF08xmlL","edit":{"x":60,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":"An image of two orange triangles on a reflective orange surface. AI-Generated Misinformation blog post. How to detect Harmful Narratives in LLMs","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afk0Q8BOoF08xmlL_AI-GeneratedMisinformation-DetectHarmfulNarrativesinLLMs_1920x1200.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=360,0,1200,1200\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afk0Q8BOoF08xmlL","edit":{"x":360,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ask an AI tool what your brand is known for, and the answer may look credible, while still containing distortions.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI systems could position a discontinued product as current. They might frame a minor customer complaint as a defining weakness or name a competitor in connection with your business for no legitimate reason.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"None of that needs to go viral to affect perception.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That’s what makes AI-generated misinformation different from older reputation threats. It enters through synthesized answers people use to compare vendors or make buying decisions. It’s bad enough when a model gets a fact wrong. But repeating weak or inaccurate signals to the point it becomes the narrative carries a heavy, costly weight. ","spans":[{"start":233,"end":338,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s a reputation issue that communications teams can’t treat as incidental.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Contents","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What is AI-Generated Misinformation?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Harmful Narratives Form in LLMs","spans":[{"start":0,"end":35,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why Misinformation Is Harder to Spot in AI Answers","spans":[{"start":0,"end":50,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Types of Harmful Narratives Brands Should Watch For","spans":[{"start":0,"end":55,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"A Framework to Detect Harmful Narratives in LLMs","spans":[{"start":0,"end":48,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How to Respond to AI-Driven Misinformation","spans":[{"start":0,"end":42,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Role of UGC in Correcting Distorted Narratives","spans":[{"start":0,"end":50,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Meltwater Helps Detect Narrative Risk","spans":[{"start":0,"end":41,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Building a Narrative Risk Strategy","spans":[{"start":0,"end":34,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Future of Reputation Management in AI","spans":[{"start":0,"end":41,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQs about AI-Generated Misinformation","spans":[{"start":0,"end":38,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What is AI-Generated Misinformation?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI-generated misinformation includes misleading, outdated, or distorted information produced or reinforced by AI systems.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sometimes it looks obvious, like fabricated statistics without clear sources or invented partnerships. AI might also hallucinate product claims that don’t exist.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"More often, it shows up in subtler ways.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI systems might say that a software company is “frequently criticized for poor onboarding” based on a handful of forum complaints from years ago, even when current customer sentiment says otherwise. The output contains fragments of truth, but the synthesis changes the meaning.","spans":[{"start":200,"end":277,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Most misinformation generated through LLMs is not intentional deception. Instead, it comes from reconstructing uneven inputs, like old media coverage, edge cases, singular reviews that don’t account for the big picture, or third-party summaries. ","spans":[{"start":96,"end":124,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When AI picks up on the wrong patterns and returns them as authoritative answers, it can distort how a market understands a brand.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":130,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Harmful Narratives Form in LLMs","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A weak signal repeated often enough can begin behaving like a consensus.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Consider a product launch that triggers complaints about onboarding friction. The issue gets resolved quickly. But dozens of posts remain indexed and cited.","spans":[{"start":125,"end":155,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Months later, AI summaries start citing “difficult onboarding” as a common drawback.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Inputs may include user forums, reviews, syndicated media coverage, comparison sites, and aggregated content. They may not be decisive by themselves alone. But together, they can harden into something larger.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why Misinformation is Harder To Spot in AI Answers","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Traditional misinformation looks like a false claim you can identify and challenge. AI-generated misinformation is harder to detect because it may appear inside polished, balanced answers that seem credible upon first read. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":26,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YL7VeRAAACQAKC8x","type":"blog_post","tags":["site"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"misinformation--disinformation-what-is-it-and-how-do-you-protect-your-brand-against-it","first_publication_date":"2021-06-08T02:29:42+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T07:18:38+0000","uid":"what-is-misinformation","url":"/en/blog/what-is-misinformation","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Fact-checking alone is no longer enough; brands must now also detect distortion hiding inside partial truths in synthesized answers.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":132,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For example, an AI answer comparing vendors may accurately summarize your pricing model and product strengths, then insert an outdated claim about implementation risk drawn from old complaints.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Because the answer looks balanced, the error hides inside otherwise credible info.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Citation transparency makes this harder. In many AI systems, users don’t see which sources shaped the synthesis. Even when links appear, the attribution may be incomplete or mismatched. That makes distortion harder to isolate before it spreads.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":21,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Types of Harmful Narratives Brands Should Watch For","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Every distorted narrative carries a different risk. These are the ones brands should watch for first:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Outdated positioning","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Incorrect associations","spans":[{"start":0,"end":22,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Exaggerated negatives","spans":[{"start":0,"end":21,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Missing context","spans":[{"start":0,"end":15,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Fabricated claims","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Outdated positioning","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Legacy narratives outlive strategy changes.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A company that moved to the enterprise years ago may still appear in AI summaries as a niche or low-cost provider. Such a claim affects category perception and could dissuade your target audience.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Incorrect associations","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands can become linked with the wrong competitors, use cases, sectors, or controversies.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For example, we have seen fintech firms become associated with crypto volatility, simply because source content overlapped. Association drift can alter buyer assumptions before a sales conversation starts.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Exaggerated negatives","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Small issues can become defining attributes: a limited recall, a temporary outage, or a support complaint trend that lasted for weeks.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Repeated enough, those can show up as persistent brand weaknesses, even when the initial issues resolve.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Missing context","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Partial truths can cause more damage than fabricated ones.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An AI answer may mention litigation without noting dismissal. It may reference layoffs without talking about an acquisition. This context loss changes meaning.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Fabricated claims","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Hallucinations still matter, and they’re still common despite improvements in AI models. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"False executive names, nonexistent certifications, product capabilities that don’t exist, or company awards or achievements become AI’s truth if repeated often enough. These tend to demand a faster response because the error is clear and material.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"A Framework to Detect Harmful Narratives in LLMs","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Narrative risk is difficult to manage without a repeatable detection process. These steps help separate isolated anomalies from patterns worth responding to. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Step 1: Audit AI outputs regularly","spans":[{"start":0,"end":34,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Step 2: Identify recurring narrative patterns","spans":[{"start":0,"end":45,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Step 3: Trace back to source signals","spans":[{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Step 4: Assess risk level","spans":[{"start":0,"end":25,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Step 5: Prioritize response","spans":[{"start":0,"end":27,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 1: Audit AI outputs regularly","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI output reviews should function like ongoing monitoring. Instead of catching isolated errors, your goal is to understand whether distortions are repeating often enough to form a narrative.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That starts with running consistent prompts across multiple systems: ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What is [brand] known for?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What are common criticisms of [brand]?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How does [brand] compare with competitors?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Then run those same prompts again over time. The value is in repetition. You are looking for language that persists or criticisms that begin appearing as standard descriptors rather than singular observations. Some misinformation detection solutions offer automated monitoring that can save you a lot of time.","spans":[{"start":215,"end":249,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aeZ-uhIAACwABgwn","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-misinformation-detection-software-solutions-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2026-05-07T17:04:23+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T07:16:29+0000","uid":"misinformation-detection-software","url":"/en/blog/misinformation-detection-software","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about LLM monitoring, LLM sentiment analysis, and brand KPIs for LLM monitoring","spans":[{"start":0,"end":95,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":3,"type":"strong"},{"start":22,"end":36,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEZ7REAACkASBji","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-brand-mentions-how-to-track-key-metrics--more","first_publication_date":"2026-01-23T00:38:25+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-29T22:51:39+0000","uid":"how-to-track-llm-brand-mentions","url":"/en/blog/how-to-track-llm-brand-mentions","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":38,"end":60,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTxTDBEAACgAXlwR","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-sentiment-analysis-complete-guide--implementation","first_publication_date":"2025-12-29T18:58:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-01-09T08:19:40+0000","uid":"llm-sentiment-analysis","url":"/en/blog/llm-sentiment-analysis","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":66,"end":95,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aVQEkBEAACkAgniH","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-metrics--kpis-how-to-measure--improve-ai-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-08T20:07:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:37:19+0000","uid":"llm-metrics-and-kpis","url":"/en/blog/llm-metrics-and-kpis","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 2: Identify recurring narrative patterns","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"One odd answer may not mean much; it’s the repeated phrasing that deserves attention.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If multiple models begin citing the same criticism or framing, even with different wording, there may be a narrative taking shape.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When this happens, your monitoring shifts into risk detection.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 3: Trace back to source signals","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Once a pattern appears, the next goal is to find what’s reinforcing it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sometimes the source is easy to spot, such as a spike in negative mentions after a product launch. Other times, it emerges through changes in media tone or recurring complaints in reviews and support communities.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A distortion driven by earned media requires a different response than one fueled by customer conversation. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s media intelligence and social listening help teams map where those signals originate, which makes response decisions much more precise.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":147,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":12,"end":30,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/media-intelligence","target":"_blank"}},{"start":35,"end":51,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/social-listening","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 4: Assess risk level","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not every distortion needs an active response, which is why risk assessment matters.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Focus on whether the misinformation is likely to affect perception or decision-making. An outdated product description may call for monitoring, while a false security concern in AI-generated comparisons could require immediate correction.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Evaluate impact and spread separately. A narrative can be highly damaging without broad reach yet, just as a widely repeated distortion may have limited consequence. Those are different response problems.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 5: Prioritize response","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"After assessing risk, the response should match the nature of the narrative.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When a harmful narrative begins influencing coverage, customer perception, or trust, the response may need coordination across PR, content, support, and sometimes product teams.","spans":[{"start":54,"end":73,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Zaf76RMAACcApjZj","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-brand-perception-and-how-to-measure-it","first_publication_date":"2024-02-26T16:11:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:13:57+0000","uid":"brand-perception","url":"/en/blog/brand-perception","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How to Respond to AI-Driven Misinformation","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You cannot directly edit an LLM’s outputs, but you can influence the signals feeding them.","spans":[{"start":55,"end":77,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Start with improving source material. Specific, updated content tends to travel farther into synthesized outputs than vague messaging.","spans":[{"start":11,"end":36,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Corrective UGC also matters. Reviews and community responses often move faster than owned channels.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"},{"start":11,"end":14,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YjOXLRIAACgAcVOg","type":"blog_post","tags":["blog"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ugc-guide-what-is-user-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2022-05-11T04:00:22+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-10T07:07:31+0000","uid":"ugc-user-generated-content","url":"/en/blog/ugc-user-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"And sometimes, the right response is addressing the underlying issue. If implementation complaints keep appearing because the experience is broken, the problem is not the narrative alone. The model may be exposing something real.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Role of UGC In Correcting Distorted Narratives","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"User-generated content can fuel harmful narratives, but it can also help correct them.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":22,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YjOXLRIAACgAcVOg","type":"blog_post","tags":["blog"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ugc-guide-what-is-user-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2022-05-11T04:00:22+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-10T07:07:31+0000","uid":"ugc-user-generated-content","url":"/en/blog/ugc-user-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A burst of complaints during a service disruption may distort perception well beyond the incident itself. But sustained corrective feedback after resolving the issue can begin changing the pattern those narratives draw from.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"What changes narratives over time is rarely isolated positive commentary. It is repeated, credible signals moving in a different direction. That’s one reason UGC monitoring belongs inside reputation strategy, not solely within social programs.","spans":[{"start":80,"end":106,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Meltwater Helps Detect Narrative Risk","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Narrative risk is difficult to manage when teams only see fragments; Meltwater helps connect those fragments.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Media monitoring can show whether the tone around a brand is changing across outlets. Social listening can reveal whether complaints consolidate around a specific issue. Consumer intelligence can help teams distinguish isolated noise from an emerging pattern. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":16,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aS3WtxIAACcAedro","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-media-monitoring-definition-tools--strategy","first_publication_date":"2025-12-16T23:50:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-12-17T00:05:14+0000","uid":"media-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/media-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":86,"end":102,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XmtEChQAACMAjeL9","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-social-listening-the-complete-guide-for-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-05T22:51:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-02T10:56:12+0000","uid":"social-media-listening-guide","url":"/en/blog/social-media-listening-guide","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":170,"end":191,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y-0p2hUAAC0AJdA6","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"consumer-intelligence-definition--examples","first_publication_date":"2023-02-16T14:29:31+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T11:30:54+0000","uid":"consumer-intelligence-definition-examples","url":"/en/blog/consumer-intelligence-definition-examples","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"And GenAI Lens continually tests AI model outputs to understand how your brand is showing up in generated responses.","spans":[{"start":4,"end":14,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aYuZ7d0YXLCxVqpT_Screen-1080x674-37-04.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"GenAI Lens showing LLM visibility","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":2250,"height":1405},"id":"aYuZ7d0YXLCxVqpT","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Having these distinctions can help determine whether brands need to respond – and if so, how to approach it. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Source mapping also matters. If a harmful narrative originates in a niche community discussion, the response may look very different than if syndicated media coverage is reinforcing it.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":27,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As AI-generated answers influence more discovery and evaluation behavior, monitoring how brands appear in those environments is becoming an important factor in reputation intelligence.","spans":[{"start":3,"end":23,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYnKBxEAACgAonTS","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-answer-engine-optimization-aeo-the-complete-guide","first_publication_date":"2026-02-09T12:09:10+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-23T09:42:27+0000","uid":"aeo","url":"/en/blog/aeo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Building a Narrative Risk Strategy","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands typically respond to a crisis once they see the problem, but narrative risk doesn’t wait for visibility. ","spans":[{"start":17,"end":36,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZpEBdxIAAC0AHLpo","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-crisis-communication-plan-examples-strategy","first_publication_date":"2024-07-22T14:23:39+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T11:38:10+0000","uid":"crisis-communication","url":"/en/blog/crisis-communication","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Distorted narratives can form long before they trigger obvious reputational damage, which is why they require continuous monitoring rather than event-driven response. ","spans":[{"start":110,"end":131,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If a team waits until AI engines broadly repeat misinformation, much of the reputational work will be defensive.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: learn more about brand risk management","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":3,"type":"strong"},{"start":22,"end":43,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ad1jlhEAAC4Ae5sJ","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"brand-risk-management-strategies-to-protect-brand-value","first_publication_date":"2026-04-14T21:59:18+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-01T05:27:19+0000","uid":"brand-risk-management","url":"/en/blog/brand-risk-management","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Future of Reputation Management in AI","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Reputation now includes how machines interpret a brand, not only how people do. As a result, the work of PR teams is changing.","spans":[{"start":93,"end":125,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abrsthEAACkANweB","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"narrative-control-in-2026-pr-strategies-for-ai--geo","first_publication_date":"2026-03-18T18:24:18+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T12:04:54+0000","uid":"pr-strategies-ai-geo","url":"/en/blog/pr-strategies-ai-geo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Communications teams are increasingly managing what audiences say and what source ecosystems reinforce. Now, they’re also paying attention to what AI systems synthesize from both.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Some organizations still treat this as an SEO problem, but it’s broader than that. It touches brand intelligence, issue monitoring, crisis readiness, and how organizations detect risk before it enters decision-making.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater helps teams monitor those signals across media, social, and consumer conversation so they can detect narrative risk earlier and respond with better intelligence. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"See how Meltwater can help you monitor and manage emerging narrative risk when you request a demo.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":98,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Scroll-To-Form"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs about AI-Generated Misinformation","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is AI-generated misinformation?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI-generated misinformation includes misleading or outdated information that AI systems produce or reinforce. This usually happens because models synthesize flawed source inputs. The issue extends beyond factual error, as these distortions may influence perceptions.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Why do LLMs generate inaccurate information about brands?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Because they rely on patterns across available content, and those patterns may include stale coverage, repeated complaints, or conflicting signals. Weak signals can gain disproportionate narrative weight.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Can brands control what AI says about them?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not directly. They can influence the signals shaping outputs through stronger content, healthier source ecosystems, and coordinated reputation management.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How can I check what AI says about my brand?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Run recurring prompts across systems like ChatGPT, Google AI Overviews, and Claude. Compare outputs over time and look for repeated themes, not isolated anomalies.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is a harmful narrative?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A harmful narrative is a recurring, misleading theme that shapes how a market understands your brand. It can carry more influence than isolated false claims because repetition gives it credibility.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How does Meltwater help detect misinformation?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater helps teams monitor source conversations, analyze shifts in sentiment and narrative framing, trace where distortions originate, and identify patterns early enough to inform response.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"Z8h9axIAACwAzIK5","uid":"twitter-social-listening","url":"/en/blog/twitter-social-listening","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22Z8h9axIAACwAzIK5%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2025-03-28T15:41:00+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-10T07:11:21+0000","slugs":["the-ultimate-guide-to-x--twitter-social-listening"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"The Ultimate Guide to X / Twitter Social Listening","published_date":"2026-05-02","author_name":"TJ Kiely","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Social Listening"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":"X"}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"illustration of the X platform logo surrounded by different social media icons representing X / Twitter social listening","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z92ZWDxkOkZ2kJEu_Landing-Page-1920x1200-twitter-social-listening.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=60,0,1800,1200\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"Z92ZWDxkOkZ2kJEu","edit":{"x":60,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z92ZWDxkOkZ2kJEu_Landing-Page-1920x1200-twitter-social-listening.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=360,0,1200,1200\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"Z92ZWDxkOkZ2kJEu","edit":{"x":360,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Twitter (now X) continues to be a mouthpiece for businesses and brands. Its rapid-fire, no-holds-barred reputation allows its users to share anything and everything — and they don’t hold back. If you’re using Twitter social listening, you can capture the fine details of what people are saying on Twitter and use it to your advantage.","spans":[{"start":217,"end":233,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XmtEChQAACMAjeL9","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-social-listening-the-complete-guide-for-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-05T22:51:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-02T10:56:12+0000","uid":"social-media-listening-guide","url":"/en/blog/social-media-listening-guide","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":243,"end":267,"type":"strong"},{"start":309,"end":333,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Social listening on Twitter gives you a front row seat to every conversation you care about. People post just about anything here, like real-time updates, opinions, breaking news, and other trending topics. Having these conversations all in one place makes it easy to keep your ear to the ground, especially if people are talking about your business.","spans":[{"start":336,"end":341,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here’s how you can use Twitter’s real-time advantage to stay ahead of the curve.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What Is X / Twitter Social Listening?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":37,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why Does X / Twitter Social Listening Matter?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":45,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What Are the Top Tools for X / Twitter Social Listening?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":56,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What Are the Best Strategies for Effective X / Twitter Social Listening?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":72,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Can Meltwater Support Your X / Twitter Social Listening Strategy?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":69,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What Is X / Twitter Social Listening?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Twitter social listening lets you monitor millions of conversations in real time on Twitter. Using social listening and analytics tools, you can set up searches for keywords, brand names, or phrases you care about, then get alerts whenever someone posts about those topics.","spans":[{"start":34,"end":67,"type":"strong"},{"start":99,"end":135,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/social-listening","target":"_blank"}},{"start":165,"end":198,"type":"strong"},{"start":220,"end":230,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z8h7iRsAHJWomJqb_usingamagnifyingglassonareport.jpeg?auto=format,compress","alt":"using a magnifying glass on a report","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":948,"height":632},"id":"Z8h7iRsAHJWomJqb","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Twitter listening is one piece of the much bigger social listening puzzle. It’s specific to the Twitter platform, giving you insights into what Twitter users are discussing.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Since Twitter has a highly engaged user base, it’s worthwhile to see what’s trending here. You’ll need to tailor your social listening strategy to the platform’s format and audience to get the best read on conversations.","spans":[{"start":20,"end":44,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"TIP: Download our free Definitive Guide to Social Listening for a deep-dive on the many benefits and use-cases of social listening solutions!","spans":[{"start":0,"end":141,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":4,"type":"strong"},{"start":23,"end":59,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnocURIAADOumkUQ","type":"resource","tags":["site","MoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-definitive-guide-to-social-listening","first_publication_date":"2020-05-15T07:50:11+0000","last_publication_date":"2024-10-15T08:24:22+0000","uid":"listen-up-social-listening","url":"/resources/listen-up-social-listening","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why Does X / Twitter Social Listening Matter?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Information moves faster than ever. A single post has the power to shape opinions and spark emotions and new ideas. If your business isn’t actively listening, you’re not just losing out on conversations — you’re missing signals that could define your next big opportunity or warn of an impending crisis.","spans":[{"start":58,"end":100,"type":"strong"},{"start":239,"end":271,"type":"strong"},{"start":275,"end":302,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"X / Twitter ranks in the top 5 social media platforms globally (Statista, 2025). While some marketers are shifting time away from X, its role in brand discovery remains significant. X (formerly Twitter) currently has a potential advertising reach of 557 million users, and 38.1% of active users on the platform use it specifically to follow or research brands and products (Meltwater, 2026).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"strong"},{"start":64,"end":78,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.statista.com/statistics/272014/global-social-networks-ranked-by-number-of-users/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":145,"end":180,"type":"strong"},{"start":250,"end":267,"type":"strong"},{"start":273,"end":294,"type":"strong"},{"start":374,"end":389,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Adding social listening on Twitter to your social media marketing can give you an advantage in this world of instant knowledge. ","spans":[{"start":43,"end":65,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YLF7qRMAACQA8xFB","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ultimate-social-media-marketing-guide","first_publication_date":"2021-06-07T19:05:41+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T16:34:46+0000","uid":"ultimate-social-media-marketing-guide","url":"/en/blog/ultimate-social-media-marketing-guide","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here’s how.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Learn more about your customers","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"People feel comfortable sharing their real thoughts on social media platforms, especially Twitter. It contains a variety of thoughts and views (sometimes heated and controversial), brand mentions, humor, and influencer content. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tapping into Twitter conversations can give you a fuller perspective of who your customers are and what makes them tick. You’ll gain raw and real expressions from your audience, even if their posts have nothing to do with your brand.","spans":[{"start":50,"end":119,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"These honest posts can help you pinpoint your customers’ frustrations, desires, and needs, allowing you to adjust your products and positioning to meet their needs.","spans":[{"start":32,"end":89,"type":"strong"},{"start":107,"end":143,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"TIP: Check out our blog on the top Customer Intelligence platforms and tools to help you learn more about your audience and their unique needs.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":143,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":4,"type":"strong"},{"start":35,"end":76,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZRRXvxEAACgApNwK","type":"blog_post","tags":["tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-8-customer-intelligence-platforms--software-2026","first_publication_date":"2023-09-29T16:25:36+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T11:43:40+0000","uid":"customer-intelligence-platform","url":"/en/blog/customer-intelligence-platform","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Manage your brand reputation","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If people were talking about your brand (and potentially spreading misinformation or negativity), you’d want to know about it. With Twitter being a place where users keep nothing sacred, it’s the prime place to air their feelings or vent frustrations. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Your brand might end up in their crosshairs, and Twitter listening can reveal when a comment or mention might hurt your reputation. ","spans":[{"start":110,"end":130,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You can also use Twitter social listening to monitor your industry as a whole. For example, supplement companies might be impacted by new research studies on specific ingredients they use. Monitoring for these mentions can help them stay ahead of potential shifts in sentiment.","spans":[{"start":45,"end":66,"type":"strong"},{"start":233,"end":276,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"TIP: Don't miss our blog on the top brand monitoring tools to help you safeguard public sentiment towards your organization!","spans":[{"start":0,"end":124,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":4,"type":"strong"},{"start":32,"end":58,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZbKndBIAACgA-9qj","type":"blog_post","tags":["tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-10-best-brand-monitoring-tools-2026","first_publication_date":"2024-02-23T15:59:45+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:13:05+0000","uid":"brand-monitoring-tools","url":"/en/blog/brand-monitoring-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"See what your competitors are up to","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Social listening tools let you monitor your competitors as well as your own brand. Learn why other people are praising or complaining about competitors. You can use this knowledge to improve your own products or services, find where customers aren’t being properly served, and position yourself accordingly.","spans":[{"start":31,"end":81,"type":"strong"},{"start":183,"end":220,"type":"strong"},{"start":277,"end":294,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You might also get inspired by your competitors’ content. Track what they post and pay attention to how their customers respond to it (e.g., retweets, comments). This can give you an idea of what your audience might find appealing so you can create content that resonates with them.","spans":[{"start":18,"end":56,"type":"strong"},{"start":242,"end":271,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The data that Meltwater allows us to access is valuable to our research and deciding what stories we want to take on. It is integral to our objectives as a business and as Andariya.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":181,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Salma Abdalla, Co-founder, Users can connect their social accounts, set up keywords, and start getting insights in a single feed. Radarr covers the most popular social media platforms, including Twitter, so you can get a better idea of your share of voice across channels.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"strong"},{"start":59,"end":84,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z8h8WxsAHJWomJq3_sociallisteningwithRadarr.jpg?auto=format,compress","alt":"social listening with Radarr","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":700,"height":641},"id":"Z8h8WxsAHJWomJq3","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Key features:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Real-time social media monitoring","spans":[{"start":10,"end":33,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YE8dghEAACAA0n2B","type":"blog_post","tags":["editors_pick","tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-top-22-social-media-monitoring-tools-2026","first_publication_date":"2021-06-07T12:47:21+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T05:30:41+0000","uid":"top-social-media-monitoring-tools","url":"/en/blog/top-social-media-monitoring-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Image analysis","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Brand reputation management","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Audience and campaign analysis","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Unified dashboard","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Topic clustering","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The platform combines analysis, engagement, and content posting to help marketers simplify their work. You can get deeper insights into your owned media, respond to comments and DMs, and even plan and publish new content to your channels. ","spans":[{"start":22,"end":63,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Sprout Social","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sprout Social offers a user-friendly interface to set up topic tracking. You don’t need experience with social listening or Boolean queries to start collecting insights. Users can keep tabs on everything from post engagement to customer feedback and sentiments from within the same tool. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"strong"},{"start":77,"end":139,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z8h8rRsAHJWomJrF_SproutSocialfortopictracking.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Sprout Social for topic tracking","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":700,"height":604},"id":"Z8h8rRsAHJWomJrF","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Key features:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Competitor analysis","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Event monitoring","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"User-friendly interface","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Trend tracking","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sprout Social will provide alerts when someone mentions your keyword or when there’s a spike in mentions, which might indicate a brewing crisis or trend. It’s also a highly visual tool, allowing you to make sense of your data in different ways. For example, you can generate word clouds to spot your most popular keywords or mentions at a glance.","spans":[{"start":27,"end":33,"type":"strong"},{"start":127,"end":143,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZpEBdxIAAC0AHLpo","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-crisis-communication-plan-examples-strategy","first_publication_date":"2024-07-22T14:23:39+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T11:38:10+0000","uid":"crisis-communication","url":"/en/blog/crisis-communication","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":173,"end":184,"type":"strong"},{"start":266,"end":286,"type":"strong"},{"start":305,"end":345,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Take a look at other Sprout Social alternatives.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":52,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aVQXwxEAACoAgpUC","type":"blog_post","tags":["alternatives"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-7-best-sprout-social-alternatives-compared-2026","first_publication_date":"2025-12-31T18:38:06+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-07T07:33:40+0000","uid":"sprout-social-alternatives","url":"/en/blog/sprout-social-alternatives","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Keyhole","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Keyhole by Muck Rack is a social media analytics tool that includes social listening as a feature. Like other social listening tools, Keyhole lets you select keywords and brand mentions to monitor across your channels. You can spot what’s trending, keep an eye on competitors, and learn more about the topics your audience cares about.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"strong"},{"start":26,"end":53,"type":"strong"},{"start":151,"end":166,"type":"strong"},{"start":171,"end":185,"type":"strong"},{"start":227,"end":247,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Xo7ythAAAB8AK3zi","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"social-media-trends-2025-what-you-need-to-know","first_publication_date":"2020-05-04T23:52:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-01-09T12:41:59+0000","uid":"social-media-trends","url":"/en/blog/social-media-trends","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Keyhole also includes audience and sentiment analysis. Knowing the context behind mentions is key in crafting an appropriate response. Compared to other social listening tools, this one is a bit limited in terms of the networks it monitors. It currently supports Twitter, Instagram, TikTok, Facebook, and YouTube, along with non-social media like blogs and forums.","spans":[{"start":22,"end":53,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. BuzzSumo","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"BuzzSumo specializes in trending topic investigation. It excels in helping you develop content based on what’s already popular. You can tap into people’s conversations to know what to post and when to share it with your audience.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"},{"start":79,"end":94,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/Z8h8_xsAHJWomJrZ_BuzzSumofortrendingtopicinvestigation.jpg?auto=format,compress","alt":"BuzzSumo for trending topic investigation","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":948,"height":737},"id":"Z8h8_xsAHJWomJrZ","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Key features:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Brand alerts","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Media monitoring","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Content discovery","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Free trial","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"BuzzSumo isn’t specific to Twitter, but it does capture content and insights from the platform and other social networks. You can see what others are posting or saying about competitors, relevant products, or other trends and see how those mentions evolve over time. It’s also a handy tool for finding journalists or influencers in your niche who can help to amplify your voice.","spans":[{"start":48,"end":76,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What Are the Best Strategies for Effective X / Twitter Social Listening?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s not just about the social listening tool you use, but also how you use it to get results. Twitter social listening tools often come with a learning curve and multiple ways you can use them.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here are some helpful hints to get you started.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Set specific goals for Twitter listening","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Twitter social listening can help you accomplish many things: monitoring competitors, improving brand reputation, shaping your content strategy, and finding new trends, for example. The key is to know what you’re looking for so you can spot those insights right away and act on them.","spans":[{"start":62,"end":168,"type":"strong"},{"start":96,"end":112,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZYHcjhEAACoAMhUg","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"brand-reputation-measurement-definition-tools-models","first_publication_date":"2024-01-02T17:56:22+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-12T08:17:46+0000","uid":"reputation-measurement","url":"/en/blog/reputation-measurement","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":127,"end":143,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XsJrIxEAAIuv77jy","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","site"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-an-effective-content-marketing-strategy-content-calendar-template","first_publication_date":"2020-05-18T11:07:06+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-09T11:19:47+0000","uid":"keys-to-effective-content-marketing-strategy","url":"/en/blog/keys-to-effective-content-marketing-strategy","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Be specific about what you want to accomplish with Twitter listening. This will help you track the right keywords and use the data you collect. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Use advanced filters to focus on relevant conversations","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When you set up keywords to track on Twitter, you might get a tsunami of data telling you more than you need to know. Learn how to use your Twitter listening tool’s filter options so you can skip right to the important stuff.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For example, Meltwater lets you filter your data in different ways. Plus, you can get spelled-out insights to know what the data means. It’s like reading a report with the juiciest info upfront instead of relying on graphs and numbers alone.","spans":[{"start":13,"end":66,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Engage with users strategically and authentically","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When the opportunity arises, be prepared to respond to comments and brand mentions. Engaging directly with your audience shows you’re paying attention, plus it helps clarify questions or comments.","spans":[{"start":29,"end":82,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Pay close attention to feedback or negative opinions. These can be opportunities to set the record straight and reposition your brand in a positive light.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Can Meltwater Support Your X / Twitter Social Listening Strategy?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For social listening, Meltwater provides a comprehensive solution in a single platform. Gain insights into your Twitter performance and monitor mentions that matter to your brand. You’ll get alerts when someone posts content about the keywords you’re tracking, along with insights on why those posts might be worth a look.","spans":[{"start":22,"end":86,"type":"strong"},{"start":112,"end":152,"type":"strong"},{"start":191,"end":197,"type":"strong"},{"start":272,"end":280,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater infuses social listening with end-to-end social media management, allowing you to run your content strategy seamlessly. Identify topics your audience cares about, create content on those topics, and schedule posts to go live at optimal times. Track your content’s performance, engage directly with your followers, and build up your brand image, all with AI-powered insights that make an impact.","spans":[{"start":18,"end":74,"type":"strong"},{"start":129,"end":145,"type":"strong"},{"start":173,"end":187,"type":"strong"},{"start":209,"end":223,"type":"strong"},{"start":253,"end":273,"type":"strong"},{"start":287,"end":302,"type":"strong"},{"start":328,"end":347,"type":"strong"},{"start":364,"end":383,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs for Twitter (X) Social Listening","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Why does Twitter social listening matter for brands?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"X has a potential advertising reach of 557 million users, and 38.1% of active users on the platform use it specifically to follow or research brands and products. Actively listening on Twitter helps you spot opportunities, manage your reputation, and stay ahead of trends before they escalate.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What are some of the best tools for Twitter social listening?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Top tools include Meltwater, Radarr, Sprout Social, Keyhole, and BuzzSumo, each offering features like real-time alerts, sentiment analysis, and keyword tracking. Meltwater processes over 1.3 billion social media posts daily and uses AI-powered insights to surface what matters most.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Why is X / Twitter particularly valuable for brand reputation management?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"X is one of the few platforms where users openly share unfiltered opinions, complaints, and frustrations in public, making it the first place negative sentiment about your brand is likely to appear. Monitoring for brand mentions and related keywords gives you the chance to respond before a single post has the opportunity to shape wider opinion.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you avoid information overload when listening on X / Twitter?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The volume of content on X means you can quickly end up with more data than you can use, which is why using advanced filters is essential. Getting specific about your goals upfront determines which keywords are worth tracking and helps you skip straight to the insights that actually matter.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you choose the right keywords to track on X / Twitter?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The right keywords depend on what you are trying to achieve, whether that is managing your brand reputation, tracking competitors, or spotting emerging trends. Starting with your brand name, product names, and industry terms gives you a solid foundation, and you can refine from there based on what the data surfaces.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How often should you review your X / Twitter social listening data?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For fast-moving topics like brand reputation or crisis detection, checking in daily or even in real time is important. For broader goals like content strategy or competitor analysis, a weekly or monthly review gives you enough data to spot meaningful patterns rather than reacting to individual posts.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What types of brands benefit most from X / Twitter social listening?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Any brand with an active audience on X stands to benefit, but it is particularly valuable for brands in industries where news, opinion, and public sentiment move quickly, such as consumer goods, tech, media, and financial services. Because X users tend to share unfiltered opinions, it gives brands access to honest, real-time feedback that other channels often do not surface.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Learn more about Meltwater for X / Twitter social listening when you request a demo by filling out the form below!","spans":[{"start":0,"end":114,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"ZcZBqhEAAC0Aq17H","uid":"influencer-marketing-competitor-audit","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing-competitor-audit","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22ZcZBqhEAAC0Aq17H%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["klear"],"first_publication_date":"2024-03-18T08:19:21+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T06:53:01+0000","slugs":["how-to-perform-influencer-marketing-competitor-audit"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"How and more brands are working with influencers every year. Competition is fiercer than ever, as 89% of companies worked with a content creator or influencer in 2025—a massive increase from just 50% in 2024 (Hubspot, 2026). Staying on top of your influencer marketing game means staying on top of your competition. In fact, it is a common belief that competition keeps you on your toes and will push you to achieve more. That is why it is important to perform regular influencer marketing competitor audits.","spans":[{"start":99,"end":115,"type":"strong"},{"start":197,"end":209,"type":"strong"},{"start":210,"end":223,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.hubspot.com/state-of-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":249,"end":269,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YKX8zxEAAB4ArQV7","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-guide-strategy-platforms--roi","first_publication_date":"2021-06-30T14:00:46+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:55:05+0000","uid":"influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An influencer marketing competitor audit will help you dissect your competitor’s influencer marketing strategy, as well as present a greater vision of your industry’s ecosystem. It will allow you to segment your competitor’s target audience, discover key values, and identify gaps in the market. To help get you started, we have created a free Competitor Audit Template to help guide you on your influencer
