efforts and partnerships of competing brands within a specific industry or niche.","spans":[{"start":45,"end":101,"type":"strong"},{"start":72,"end":92,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YKX8zxEAAB4ArQV7","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-guide-strategy-platforms--roi","first_publication_date":"2021-06-30T14:00:46+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:55:05+0000","uid":"influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":105,"end":117,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The purpose of this audit is to gain insights into how competitors are leveraging influencer marketing, identify successful strategies and partnerships, and uncover opportunities for differentiation and improvement.","spans":[{"start":32,"end":45,"type":"strong"},{"start":104,"end":134,"type":"strong"},{"start":157,"end":178,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How To Perform an Influencer Marketing Competitor Audit","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"While these two new features are great on their own, they are extremely useful when performing an influencer marketing competitor audit.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 1: Review Competitor’s Social Media Presence","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Review your competitor’s overall social media presence. Look at the main channels that your competitor uses and the recent influencer campaigns that they have run. Identify a successful campaign that can be used to evaluate the brand’s influencer marketing strategy.","spans":[{"start":68,"end":81,"type":"strong"},{"start":116,"end":143,"type":"strong"},{"start":123,"end":143,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y8lxAhIAAC4AZDaj","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"11-examples-of-the-best-influencer-marketing-campaigns","first_publication_date":"2023-02-15T16:08:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2024-08-08T08:57:23+0000","uid":"influencer-campaign-examples","url":"/en/blog/influencer-campaign-examples","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":164,"end":194,"type":"strong"},{"start":236,"end":265,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnJEwBIAACcAd4XE","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-marketing-strategy","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:36:56+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:49:21+0000","uid":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","url":"/en/blog/how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 2: Discover Influencers That Have Collaborated With Competitors","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/8b3b9715-d973-4a50-94b9-f2f562a00611_Brand-Search-General-1.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"Lululemon brand search","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1200,"height":871},"id":"ZcZI6hEAAC4Aq2tp","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Using the Meltwater Influencer Marketing suite brand search filter, search for your competitors, and discover which influencers the brand has collaborated with. Also, look at their tier strategy. Marketers reported the most success with micro-influencers (10K to 100K followers) in 2025, while nano-influencers (1K to 10K) dropped to the least effective influencer type (Hubspot, 2026). Review collaboration content and identify the goals of your competitors and the type of audience they are trying to connect with. This can offer a better understanding of your competitors and what type of market they are penetrating.","spans":[{"start":10,"end":46,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":219,"end":278,"type":"strong"},{"start":338,"end":370,"type":"strong"},{"start":371,"end":384,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.hubspot.com/state-of-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":387,"end":415,"type":"strong"},{"start":467,"end":515,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 3: Perform a Data-Driven Review of Your Competitor’s Influencers","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Take a deep dive into your competitor’s influencer metrics. Understand the type of influencer the brand is collaborating with, and identify success criteria your competitor uses for selection. Additionally, leverage Meltwater's vast content portfolio and review all brands that an influencer has collaborated with. This will help you understand why a specific influencer may be appealing to your competitor, and offer inspiration for future collaborations.","spans":[{"start":40,"end":58,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Xmli7RIAACAAf2gQ","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu","LKF"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-measurement-kpis-metrics-roi","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:35:31+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-30T12:14:03+0000","uid":"measuring-influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/measuring-influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":75,"end":93,"type":"strong"},{"start":130,"end":156,"type":"strong"},{"start":254,"end":313,"type":"strong"},{"start":345,"end":387,"type":"strong"},{"start":418,"end":455,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"51.2% of marketers prioritize performance tracking, but the reliance on vanity metrics, such as engagement and views, indicates a lingering gap in fully embracing ROI-driven frameworks. Experts advocate for a paradigm shift toward performance-based contracts and long-term strategic alignment. They warn against over-reliance on vanity metrics, urging a focus on tangible outcomes like sales, brand loyalty, and community sentiment (Influencer Marketing Hub, 2025).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"},{"start":72,"end":86,"type":"strong"},{"start":231,"end":292,"type":"strong"},{"start":386,"end":391,"type":"strong"},{"start":393,"end":406,"type":"strong"},{"start":412,"end":432,"type":"strong"},{"start":433,"end":463,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://influencermarketinghub.com/influencer-marketing-benchmark-report/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"My favourite feature of the tool is the deep insights we can get about each influencer, such as follower demographics, engagement rates and true reach. It gives the team confidence in selecting the best influencers","spans":[{"start":0,"end":214,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Angelique Hernandez, Asia Marketing Director, Yves Rocher","spans":[{"start":0,"end":19,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.linkedin.com/in/angelique-eleonore-hernandez-79441887/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":0,"end":57,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}},{"start":46,"end":57,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/customer-stories/yves-rocher","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 4: Identify Values That Your Competitor is Promoting","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/93dcc5a0-4e61-4359-aa13-28e827d6c422_Brand-Search-General-2.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"Jennifer Simone Fitness brand search influencer marketing competitor audit","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1024,"height":743},"id":"ZcZJIREAACkAq2vM","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Look at several examples of your competitor’s campaign content. Identify values that the brand is conveying through the content. Understand how these values fit into your industry ecosystem, and if you should be participating in similar discourse with your audience.","spans":[{"start":16,"end":62,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Step 5: Evaluate The Network Your Competitor Engages With","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Identify which demographics your competitor is targeting. Use Meltwater's audience technology to review your competitor’s audience. Identify key demographic trends including gender, age, location, and interests, and pinpoint untapped markets and areas of penetration for your brand.","spans":[{"start":131,"end":163,"type":"strong"},{"start":216,"end":241,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Takeaways","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Determine key lessons from competitor audits. What can your team take away from the review, and what can be successfully applied to your strategy?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Influencer Marketing Competitor Audit: Step-by-Step Framework","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ctable class=\"table-primary\"\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eAction\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhat to Look For\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eStrategic Outcome\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReview competitor social media presence\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKey channels used; recent campaigns\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eIdentify a benchmark campaign to evaluate\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDiscover competitor influencer collaborations\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInfluencer tiers; content style; brand fit\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eUnderstand which audiences competitors are targeting\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAnalyse influencer performance metrics\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSales impact, brand loyalty, community sentiment (not vanity metrics)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eIdentify the selection criteria competitors use\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIdentify values being promoted\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMessaging themes; campaign content tone\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAssess whether your brand should enter similar conversations\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEvaluate audience demographics\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAge, gender, location, interests\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePinpoint untapped markets for your brand\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs about Influencer Marketing Competitor Audits","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is an influencer marketing competitor audit?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An influencer marketing competitor audit is a structured analysis of how competing brands are using influencer partnerships within your industry. It helps you understand their strategy, identify successful collaborations, and uncover gaps your brand can exploit.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Who should conduct an influencer marketing competitor audit?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":60,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Any brand that is actively investing in influencer marketing should be conducting regular competitor audits. This includes social media managers, influencer marketing specialists, and broader marketing teams who are responsible for strategy and campaign planning.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What metrics should I focus on when auditing a competitor's influencer activity?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":80,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Rather than relying on vanity metrics like views and engagement rates, the focus should be on performance indicators tied to real business outcomes — specifically sales conversion, brand loyalty signals, and community sentiment. These provide a more accurate picture of whether a competitor's influencer strategy is actually working.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What are common influencer marketing competitor analysis mistakes?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":66,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"One of the most common mistakes is focusing too heavily on vanity metrics like views and engagement, rather than indicators that reflect real business outcomes such as sales and brand loyalty. Another is treating a competitor audit as a one-time exercise rather than an ongoing practice, which means missing new collaborations and emerging trends.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you find out which influencers your competitors are working with?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":72,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brand search features within influencer marketing platforms allow you to search a competitor's name and surface influencers who have tagged or collaborated with that brand. Reviewing this content reveals the types of creators they favor and the audiences they're trying to reach.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What tools are used in an influencer marketing audit?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A strong influencer marketing audit relies on tools that can surface competitor collaborations, analyze influencer metrics, and break down audience demographics at scale. Meltwater's Influencer Marketing suite offers brand search functionality that lets you search a competitor's name and discover the influencers they have worked with, along with detailed audience insights.","spans":[{"start":171,"end":209,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How can Meltwater's AI-powered insights help with my influencer marketing competitor audit?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Manual audits are no longer sufficient to keep pace with the market, as Meltwater’s AI-powered insights process over 1.3 billion social media posts, news articles, and blog posts every single day to ensure no competitor mention or influencer collaboration is missed (Meltwater, 2026). Meltwater is always releasing new features to ensure that our influencer marketing platform is providing a relevant experience that aligns with market trends. To learn more about how our features can support your brand’s goals, schedule a call with one of Meltwater’s influencer marketing experts:","spans":[{"start":72,"end":128,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}},{"start":72,"end":128,"type":"strong"},{"start":267,"end":282,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":true}},{"id":"ZbpNgRIAAC4ABxNW","uid":"influencer-management","url":"/en/blog/influencer-management","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22ZbpNgRIAAC4ABxNW%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["klear"],"first_publication_date":"2024-02-23T16:46:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-02T12:29:31+0000","slugs":["influencer-management-how-to-build-successful-relationships"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[{"id":"Z1sKWBEAACcARRTN","type":"blog_post","lang":"de","uid":"influencer-management"}],"data":{"title":"Influencer Management: How To Build Successful Relationships","published_date":"2026-05-02","author_name":"Mark Jackson","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Influencer Marketing"}],"social_networks":[],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"3D Illustration of influencer icons with a like to showcase influencer management","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/b9f08f8a-5d60-4927-8042-375a05bfd320_influencer-management-Blog.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=140,0,1220,813\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"ZcYvxhEAACsAq0KI","edit":{"x":-221,"y":0,"zoom":1.5744157441574416,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":"3D Illustration of influencer icons with a like to showcase influencer management","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/b9f08f8a-5d60-4927-8042-375a05bfd320_influencer-management-Blog.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=343,0,813,813\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"ZcYvxhEAACsAq0KI","edit":{"x":-456,"y":0,"zoom":1.3284132841328413,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer marketing is the key to brand awareness, growth, and sustainable success. It allows you to get in front of audiences that are likely to be interested in what you do. Plus, a recommendation from someone people trust can give you major street cred. Influencer-led discovery is vital for conversion, as 72% of internet users do brand research on social media before making a purchase (Meltwater, 2026).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YKX8zxEAAB4ArQV7","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-guide-strategy-platforms--roi","first_publication_date":"2021-06-30T14:00:46+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:55:05+0000","uid":"influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":35,"end":50,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnC_xhIAACcAcKI_","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-ultimate-brand-awareness-guide-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-06T17:51:03+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:09:03+0000","uid":"brand-awareness-guide","url":"/en/blog/brand-awareness-guide","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":311,"end":366,"type":"strong"},{"start":393,"end":408,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But first, you need a way to connect with influencers and build a strong relationship with them. That’s where an influencer management strategy comes in. Implementing an influencer management program can streamline this process.","spans":[{"start":29,"end":85,"type":"strong"},{"start":170,"end":199,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Just like with your customers, relationships don’t happen automatically. They’re the products of careful nurturing, marketing, ongoing attention, and beneficial terms for both parties. A well-designed influencer management program can help companies build successful relationships with their influencers.","spans":[{"start":116,"end":125,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YOCbghAAACMAU6wa","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-marketing--why-your-business-cant-do-without-it","first_publication_date":"2021-07-20T07:29:40+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-10T06:56:47+0000","uid":"what-is-marketing","url":"/en/blog/what-is-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Strong influencer relationships aren’t built on chance. Here’s how an influencer management strategy can help you create better connections.","spans":[{"start":70,"end":100,"type":"strong"},{"start":105,"end":140,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What Is Influencer Management?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":30,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why You Need to Manage Influencer Relationships","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What Is an Influencer Management Strategy?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":42,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why Is an Influencer Management Strategy Important?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":51,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"6 Tips For Successful Influencer Relationships","spans":[{"start":0,"end":46,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Meltwater Helps You Create Better Influencer Relationships","spans":[{"start":0,"end":62,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What Is Influencer Management?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer management refers to the process of identifying, engaging, and maintaining relationships with individuals who have a significant online presence and the ability to influence the opinions and purchasing decisions of their followers.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":242,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"These individuals, known as influencers, typically have a substantial following on social media platforms such as Instagram, YouTube, Twitter, or TikTok.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The goal of influencer management is to leverage the influence and reach of these individuals to promote a brand, product, or service. Influencer marketing has moved beyond \"top-of-funnel\" awareness; as creators add shoppable storefronts and links, they are becoming critical drivers of actual sales across all stages of your customer's journey (AMA, 2026).","spans":[{"start":39,"end":73,"type":"strong"},{"start":97,"end":133,"type":"strong"},{"start":306,"end":344,"type":"strong"},{"start":346,"end":355,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.ama.org/marketing-news/2026-trends-report/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":356,"end":357,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about Key Opinion Leaders (KOLs).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":5,"type":"strong"},{"start":22,"end":48,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZdiuuREAACcAC7Qw","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-are-key-opinion-leaders","first_publication_date":"2024-03-07T11:26:51+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-10-30T10:01:07+0000","uid":"key-opinion-leaders","url":"/en/blog/key-opinion-leaders","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why You Need to Manage Influencer Relationships","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In a world where anyone can be an influencer, it's more important than ever to have a strategy for managing those relationships. Whether you're looking to build brand awareness, drive traffic or sales, improve marketing, or simply create a better customer experience, influencers can play a key role.","spans":[{"start":155,"end":176,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnC_xhIAACcAcKI_","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-ultimate-brand-awareness-guide-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-06T17:51:03+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-06T11:09:03+0000","uid":"brand-awareness-guide","url":"/en/blog/brand-awareness-guide","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":247,"end":266,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XzKtoxEAACIAwgKk","type":"blog_post","tags":["LKF"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-customer-experience-definition-challenges-importance","first_publication_date":"2020-08-11T14:42:44+0000","last_publication_date":"2023-10-30T08:15:17+0000","uid":"what-is-customer-experience","url":"/en/blog/what-is-customer-experience","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But finding and nurturing those relationships takes time and effort. It's not enough to identify the right influencers – you also need to develop strategies for engaging them and tracking their impact.","spans":[{"start":88,"end":118,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XsLHIBEAAFzB8STY","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-find-influencers-for-your-brand-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-18T17:50:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T11:44:35+0000","uid":"finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","url":"/en/blog/finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":138,"end":200,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That's why more brands are discovering the benefits of influencer relationship management (IRM). Influencer relationship management tools and platforms can help you track and manage your interactions with influencers, making it easier to identify the most valuable relationships and get the most out of them.","spans":[{"start":97,"end":151,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y6tKMhIAACwABiJP","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-influencer-management-platforms--tools-2026","first_publication_date":"2022-12-29T19:43:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:54:01+0000","uid":"influencer-management-platforms-tools","url":"/en/blog/influencer-management-platforms-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":97,"end":199,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In today’s landscape, single and transactional relationships often fall flat. So, relationship-building should never be an afterthought. Building long-term relationships supports the long-term success of your brand. An effective influencer relationship management is built on trust, clear communication, and a win-win approach for both brands and influencers. It can lead to more authentic and successful campaigns. ","spans":[{"start":137,"end":214,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Pro tip for long-term influencer partnerships: Save time and resources! Working with influencers for long-term partnerships means less time doing outreach and onboarding to a whole new group. As relationships grow, the more in tune you will be with your influencers, making content creation and collaboration more streamlined. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":327,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":46,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What Is an Influencer Management Strategy?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An influencer management strategy is a documented, intentional approach to building and maintaining relationships with influencers. This strategy gives you a roadmap to follow so you can make each relationship a priority and track your results.","spans":[{"start":39,"end":130,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An influencer management strategy involves planning and executing a series of steps to effectively collaborate with influencers and leverage their reach and influence to achieve specific marketing goals. ","spans":[{"start":43,"end":65,"type":"strong"},{"start":87,"end":110,"type":"strong"},{"start":170,"end":202,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A well-crafted influencer management strategy takes into account these elements and aims to create a mutually beneficial relationship between the brand and influencers, ultimately contributing to the overall success of the marketing efforts.","spans":[{"start":92,"end":167,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why Is an Influencer Management Strategy Important?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/217dca15-5767-4bad-b432-ee58cd61d8b9_20221117_The+Best+Influencer+Management+Platforms+%26+Tools+%5B2022%5D.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"3D Illustration of a phone for influencer management","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"id":"Y6tHvxIAAC4ABh7O","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ultimately, your influencers promote your brand or products in a positive light. But without a good existing relationship, they may not feel connected to your brand enough to present you in a compelling way. Or worse, they might decide you’re not worth their time or otherwise don’t feel comfortable recommending you to their followers.","spans":[{"start":29,"end":79,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer management helps you build stronger connections with each influencer. It helps them get to know your brand on a deeper level so they can put you in the best light when promoting you. It also makes each promotion feel more genuine for their followers when influencers care about the brands they work with.","spans":[{"start":32,"end":58,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An influencer relationship management program can help you keep track of these moving parts, and it all starts with a strategy. To get a headstart on your planning, learn how to build a successful influencer marketing strategy. With a blueprint in hand, you can continue nurturing the relationship, foster loyalty via an influencer loyalty program, foster goodwill, and make it easy for them to share some brand love.","spans":[{"start":3,"end":45,"type":"strong"},{"start":59,"end":69,"type":"strong"},{"start":171,"end":226,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnJEwBIAACcAd4XE","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-marketing-strategy","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:36:56+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:49:21+0000","uid":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","url":"/en/blog/how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":321,"end":347,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Zboe6RIAAC0ABs0W","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-run-an-influencer-loyalty-program","first_publication_date":"2024-02-16T15:45:27+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-10-28T18:09:26+0000","uid":"how-to-run-an-influencer-loyalty-program","url":"/en/blog/how-to-run-an-influencer-loyalty-program","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"6 Tips For Successful Influencer Relationships","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/17d2c0dd-f13f-4c33-905f-a1f178202fbf_The+Ultimate+Guide+to+Influencer+Marketing.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"Photo of a young woman as an influencer","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"id":"ZMG16BIAAIOXXSFw","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"First time leading influencer marketing campaigns? We’ve been in your shoes and have learned some things along the way. As you’re crafting your digital influencer management approach, put the following tips into practice:","spans":[{"start":19,"end":49,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y8lxAhIAAC4AZDaj","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"11-examples-of-the-best-influencer-marketing-campaigns","first_publication_date":"2023-02-15T16:08:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2024-08-08T08:57:23+0000","uid":"influencer-campaign-examples","url":"/en/blog/influencer-campaign-examples","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Reach Out to Relevant Influencers","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s easier to build an influencer database if you start by connecting with relevant prospects. Not everyone with an audience will help your brand flourish.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The right influencer partners should share your target audience that’s most likely to care about what you do. A beauty influencer probably isn’t a great fit for a fishing brand. Make it easy for followers to connect your brand to whatever the influencer does best to build credibility and better brand recall.","spans":[{"start":4,"end":29,"type":"strong"},{"start":112,"end":129,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZGT6JxMAACwAnp61","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"50-top-beauty-influencers-to-inspire-you","first_publication_date":"2023-05-22T16:19:45+0000","last_publication_date":"2024-03-27T12:57:22+0000","uid":"top-beauty-influencers","url":"/en/blog/top-beauty-influencers","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Also, don’t confuse relevance with audience size. Even smaller followings can have a big impact on your influencer campaigns if they’re engaged and trust the influencer’s recommendations. Marketers reported the most success with micro-influencers (10K to 100K followers), finding them more effective for building deeper connections than larger-tier creators (Hubspot, 2026).","spans":[{"start":211,"end":270,"type":"strong"},{"start":359,"end":372,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.hubspot.com/state-of-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn how to find the right influencers to partner with your brand.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":72,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":5,"type":"strong"},{"start":11,"end":71,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XsLHIBEAAFzB8STY","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-find-influencers-for-your-brand-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-18T17:50:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T11:44:35+0000","uid":"finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","url":"/en/blog/finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. Set Clear Expectations","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Building good relationships means starting off on the right foot. But you also wouldn’t want to make a first impression you can’t live up to. That’s why it’s important to think about what you want your influencer relationships to look like to set the tone for the relationship moving forward. An influencer proposal template can help you clarify your needs.","spans":[{"start":171,"end":239,"type":"strong"},{"start":296,"end":324,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"ZbusNBIAACkA5JVe","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-create-an-influencer-proposal-template--example","first_publication_date":"2024-03-07T09:02:20+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:59:35+0000","uid":"influencer-proposal-template","url":"/en/blog/influencer-proposal-template","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":296,"end":324,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The next step is to outline your expectations in an influencer agreement and provide it to the influencer. Include details about payments, how you’ll evaluate their performance, contract length, and other specifics.","spans":[{"start":20,"end":45,"type":"strong"},{"start":52,"end":72,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnI8pxIAACoAd1-t","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"12-elements-of-successful-influencer-marketing-agreements","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:38:28+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:55:20+0000","uid":"influencer-marketing-agreements","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing-agreements","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Contracts work both ways. They give both sides some security and assurances that the other party will live up to certain expectations. It holds you both to higher standards, which can lead to a more fruitful relationship.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Give Influencers Creative Freedom","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Here’s a tough pill for some brands to swallow – your influencers know their audience better than you do. They're experienced content creators and know what works when it comes to promoting products and services to their followers. This is their area of expertise, so let them influence the best ways they know how.","spans":[{"start":106,"end":142,"type":"strong"},{"start":147,"end":162,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Allowing some creative freedom with your influencer campaigns can help you to get better outcomes. Remember, your influencer’s reputation is on the line, too. They’ll only present your products in a way they think will work because they wouldn’t want to risk damage to their own brand.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":30,"type":"strong"},{"start":78,"end":97,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about risk and reputation management, learn how to create a reputation management policy, and take a look at the best reputation management companies.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":166,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":5,"type":"strong"},{"start":22,"end":52,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Xml_cBIAAB8Af-jT","type":"blog_post","tags":["site","ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"risk-and-reputation-management-the-hot-topic-at-press-lounge-gathering","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T16:36:15+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-02-01T00:05:40+0000","uid":"risk-and-reputation-management-the-hot-topic-at-press-lounge-gathering","url":"/en/blog/risk-and-reputation-management-the-hot-topic-at-press-lounge-gathering","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":60,"end":104,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"X_Q0GBEAACEAPCKu","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","site","blog"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"why-you-need-a-reputation-management-strategy-and-how-to-create-one","first_publication_date":"2021-01-13T01:48:41+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-15T08:16:46+0000","uid":"reputation-management-policy-strategy","url":"/en/blog/reputation-management-policy-strategy","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":129,"end":165,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YXsObREAACIAMOIE","type":"blog_post","tags":["tools","BoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-9-online-reputation-management-companies-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2021-12-09T17:02:12+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-05T12:16:44+0000","uid":"best-online-reputation-management-companies","url":"/en/blog/best-online-reputation-management-companies","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Choose the Right Engagement Metrics","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tracking your influencer’s performance is a natural part of influencer marketing management. Given the sheer volume of different types of content from different sources, 87% of marketers globally cite monitoring brand reputation as their top goal (Meltwater, 2026). You want to know your influencers are making real connections on your behalf. This gives you a better idea of which influencers to continue partnering with and which ones to let go.","spans":[{"start":170,"end":186,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}},{"start":201,"end":246,"type":"strong"},{"start":248,"end":263,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That said, not just any metrics or KPIs give you a clear idea of your influencer’s performance. Vanity metrics like impressions and likes mean little in the grand scheme of ROI. Fake influencers rely heavily on these metrics because they think they make them look more valuable.","spans":[{"start":96,"end":149,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"A better approach to measuring the ROI of your influencer marketing is to look beneath the surface and track things like clicks, sales, conversions, website traffic, and measuring brand awareness. If your brand is steadily gaining more attention, it’s a good sign your influencer is doing their job.","spans":[{"start":21,"end":67,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Xmli7RIAACAAf2gQ","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu","LKF"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-measurement-kpis-metrics-roi","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:35:31+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-30T12:14:03+0000","uid":"measuring-influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/measuring-influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":170,"end":195,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XmpTaRQAACEAibmV","type":"blog_post","tags":["LKF"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-complete-guide-to-measuring-brand-awareness-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T17:24:15+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T11:48:25+0000","uid":"measure-brand-awareness","url":"/en/blog/measure-brand-awareness","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":205,"end":298,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. Attribute Results to the Right Influencer","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If you’re working with multiple influencers, you’ll need a way to see which ones are driving the results and which ones aren’t. One way to do this is to give them each a unique offer code to share with their audience. You can track conversions by offer code to see who’s bringing the most engagement.","spans":[{"start":66,"end":104,"type":"strong"},{"start":170,"end":187,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/18d2c306-730d-45ab-afad-c5521c0c1d12_Meltwater-social-influencers-product-banner-Easily+Manage+%26+Scale+Your+Influencer+Campaigns+With+Our+Influencer+Marketing+Hub-.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"influencer marketing hub demo Meltwater banner","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":750,"height":500},"id":"ZKQg8RIAACsA3SQW","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer marketing platforms like the Meltwater influencer marketing suite can help you keep tabs on all of your influencer campaigns from a single location. workflow","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1000,"height":625},"id":"Zbd4LRIAACgAAr9r","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"#fff"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater is a leader in social media influencer management. Our comprehensive platform gives you a single source for all your hands-on activities and even automates some on your behalf. Quickly discover influencers that make sense to work with, then nurture and manage the entire relationship with ease.","spans":[{"start":195,"end":215,"type":"strong"},{"start":251,"end":293,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs about Influencer Management","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What's the difference between influencer management and influencer marketing?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer marketing is the broader strategy of using creators to promote your brand. Influencer management is the ongoing process of building and maintaining those creator relationships so campaigns stay effective over time.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do I know which influencers are the right fit for my brand?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Look for alignment between the influencer's audience and your target customer, not just follower count. A smaller creator whose community genuinely trusts their recommendations will often outperform a large account with low engagement.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Do I need a formal contract with influencers?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Yes, even for smaller collaborations. A written agreement protects both parties by clarifying deliverables, payment terms, timelines, and content expectations before the partnership begins.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do I measure whether my influencer campaigns are actually working?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Skip vanity metrics like likes and impressions and focus on what drives business results: conversions, referral traffic, sales attributed to unique promo codes, and overall brand search lift.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Is it better to work with one influencer or many at the same time?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It depends on your goals, but long-term partnerships with a smaller group of well-aligned creators tend to be more efficient and authentic than running many one-off activations with different people.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you maintain your brand's authenticity while still giving influencers creative freedom?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Over-scripting content makes it feel like an ad rather than a genuine recommendation. Provide a brief with your key messages and brand guidelines, then let the creator decide the format, tone, and execution. How often should I be in contact with influencers I'm working with?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How often should I be in contact with influencers I'm working with?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"There's no fixed rule, but consistent touchpoints matter. Regular check-ins, campaign updates, and even informal engagement with their content helps maintain the relationship between active campaign periods.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Learn more when you schedule a demo by filling out the form below:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":66,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":true}},{"id":"Zdh0TREAACoACrYL","uid":"scaling-influencer-outreach","url":"/en/blog/scaling-influencer-outreach","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22Zdh0TREAACoACrYL%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["klear"],"first_publication_date":"2024-03-07T11:01:24+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-02T10:34:55+0000","slugs":["scaling-your-influencer-outreach-without-compromising-quality"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"Scaling Your Influencer Outreach Without Compromising Quality","published_date":"2026-05-02","author_name":"Mark Jackson","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Influencer
