talk about influencer outreach. 77.8% of companies perform their social media management in-house. Notably, brands are moving away from outsourcing influencer marketing services to build direct, long-term relationships with creators to ensure authenticity and control (Meltwater, 2026).","spans":[{"start":37,"end":103,"type":"strong"},{"start":275,"end":290,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/state-of-social-media","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Whether you are just getting your feet wet in influencer marketing or are a seasoned pro, outreach will always remain a fundamental part of the process. Once you get in the groove, naturally, you’ll want to increase your goals and start to scale. At this point, brands are often left wondering how to manage communications without spending all day drowning in email or sacrificing a personalized approach.","spans":[{"start":46,"end":66,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"YKX8zxEAAB4ArQV7","type":"blog_post","tags":["pillar"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"influencer-marketing-guide-strategy-platforms--roi","first_publication_date":"2021-06-30T14:00:46+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:55:05+0000","uid":"influencer-marketing","url":"/en/blog/influencer-marketing","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Unfortunately, there is no magic step-by-step guide for success. Instead, you’ll need to spend time planning, testing, and evaluating your email outreach strategy to find what works for you. But, there are some best practices and resources available to set you up for success.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Let’s Start With The Basics of Influencer Outreach","spans":[{"start":0,"end":50,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Be Intentional In Your Influencer Pitch","spans":[{"start":0,"end":39,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Ready to Hit Send","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Test, Optimize, Test Some More","spans":[{"start":0,"end":30,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Simple Art of The Follow-up","spans":[{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Ready To Scale Your Influencer Outreach?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":40,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Reading Tip: How to Approach Influencers Through Emails","spans":[{"start":0,"end":55,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":13,"end":55,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Zc4oOhIAACcA81_b","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","first_publication_date":"2024-02-23T16:53:10+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T11:48:57+0000","uid":"how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","url":"/en/blog/how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Let’s Start With The Basics of Influencer Outreach","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Successful influencer marketing is built on connection and authenticity. With 46.9% of marketers prioritizing community building in their partnerships, collaborations, and campaigns are about more than reach alone (Influencer Marketing Hub, 2026).","spans":[{"start":44,"end":71,"type":"strong"},{"start":78,"end":128,"type":"strong"},{"start":192,"end":213,"type":"strong"},{"start":215,"end":245,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://influencermarketinghub.com/influencer-marketing-benchmark-report/","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When brands invest in long-term, genuine relationships, that trust shows up in the content and resonates within the influencer’s community. Authentic partnerships feel aligned, not transactional — and audiences can tell. The result is stronger credibility, deeper trust, and more lasting brand impact.","spans":[{"start":12,"end":54,"type":"strong"},{"start":235,"end":269,"type":"strong"},{"start":280,"end":300,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater Influencer Marketing is useful in so many ways for us. It allows us to identify authentic influencers relevant to our brand and helps us easily communicate with our partners.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":184,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meri Hovnanyan, Social Media Associate, Picsart","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/customer-stories/picsart","target":"_blank"}},{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"So, having the right influencers on board can make or break your campaign. But outreach is a two-way street. You can spend all the time in the world compiling your perfect influencer list, but if you don't get a response, your goals will be at risk.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The Human Element:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Always remember there's a person on the other side of your email. Humans are complex, and our attention spans are fleeting. Influencers, much like everyone else, lead lives filled with responsibilities—jobs, family, and friends all vying for their attention. Before sending that email, pause and reflect on your own experiences. Consider what motivated you to engage, respond, and follow through when getting email outreach. Or, think about the instances that made you cringe, shake your head, or promptly hit delete. Invest the time to carefully craft your pitch.","spans":[{"start":16,"end":64,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Be Intentional In Your Influencer Pitch","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"First impressions matter. In email outreach, it’s important to be intentional in your first touchpoint. The urge to include every detail and brand message will creep up, but it’s important to take a step back and focus on the essentials.","spans":[{"start":50,"end":102,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As you draft your influencer outreach pitch, consider these tips:   ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Be concise and clear: A two-page email with intricate details and one-sided branded messaging can turn off an influencer. Make sure your email clearly states its purpose—whether it's a collaboration proposal, product review, or event invitation. Make the purpose evident from the very beginning.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":21,"type":"strong"},{"start":143,"end":169,"type":"strong"},{"start":246,"end":270,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Keep It Personal: It's a no-brainer, but addressing the influencer by name and showing you’ve done some research is important. While you don't need to go overboard, let them know why you think a collaboration is a good fit. Acknowledging the type of content they specialize in and how that aligns with your brand can make a significant impact.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"},{"start":41,"end":74,"type":"strong"},{"start":94,"end":112,"type":"strong"},{"start":242,"end":257,"type":"strong"},{"start":281,"end":296,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Make it Engaging: Reflecting your brand voice in the email is an engaging and strategic way to kick your relationships off on the right foot. Aligning your communication with your voice fosters a sense of authenticity from the get-go that can pave the way for a positive relationship.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":16,"type":"strong"},{"start":142,"end":217,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Include Next Steps: Provide clarity on the next steps. Whether you're looking for a response within a specific timeframe or are looking for influencers to apply through a separate link, make sure the influencer knows what’s next.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"strong"},{"start":186,"end":228,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Ready to Hit Send","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Once you’ve compiled your list of targeted influencers and have your pitch, it’s time to hit send! Luckily, there are tools available to make the process run smoothly.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Through Meltwater Influencer Marketing's email integration, you can connect your email account to seamlessly send emails directly from the platform. So you don’t have to toggle back and forth between screens and get lost in spreadsheets. Meltwater's email functionality caters to both individualized outreach and large-scale campaigns.","spans":[{"start":8,"end":38,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":68,"end":120,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":" The email functionality works for both one-off emails or mass outreach.","spans":[{"start":40,"end":54,"type":"strong"},{"start":58,"end":71,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Individual emails: For personalized, nuanced campaigns tailored to each influencer, you’ll likely want to stick to one-off emails. Within Meltwater Influencer Marketing, you can send emails directly from search results or from an influencer’s profile. So once you’ve found a great fit, you can get your outreach out the door immediately.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"},{"start":23,"end":82,"type":"strong"},{"start":138,"end":168,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/7d481895-27d2-4e44-8f79-f763e94e76a8_Meltwater+Influencer+Marketing+Screenshot.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"Meltwater Influencer Marketing Outreach","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":600,"height":300},"id":"Zdh0LREAACwACrVz","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"list-item","text":"Mass outreach: For larger-scale campaigns, you can send in bulk to streamline the process, without sacrificing personalization. We know mass outreach can get a bad reputation, but there are good and bad ways to go about it. With Meltwater Influencer Marketing, you don’t have to sacrifice personalization with this approach. With customizable email templates and custom fields, you can inject personalized touchpoints into each message. To add another layer of personalization and to improve your influencer strategy, you can set up multiple outreach campaigns and adjust your message to different groups to improve response and engagement (more details on that in the next section!)","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"},{"start":51,"end":126,"type":"strong"},{"start":229,"end":259,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":330,"end":376,"type":"strong"},{"start":497,"end":516,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XnJEwBIAACcAd4XE","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-marketing-strategy","first_publication_date":"2020-05-07T18:36:56+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:49:21+0000","uid":"how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","url":"/en/blog/how-to-build-a-successful-influencer-strategy-with-the-right-social-channels","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":533,"end":584,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Test, Optimize, Test Some More","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Once you’ve started to send your first batch of emails, the job doesn’t end there. To get the best results, there will be an ongoing cycle of review and optimization as you go. Paying close attention to responses (or lack thereof) is key to uncovering the secret sauce that works for you and getting the best response possible.","spans":[{"start":125,"end":165,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Nothing hurts more than pitching all your options at once and hearing crickets, leaving so many missed opportunities on the table. To avoid that, consider these tips:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Use Variation: Create a few versions of your pitch and diversify your outreach to see which has a better response rate. Experiment with a shorter vs. longer approach and test different subject lines to see which gets more engagement.  ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"},{"start":15,"end":36,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Segment your audience: Leverage the power of tags within Meltwater Influencer Marketing to create different segments, allowing you to tailor emails based on specific criteria (e.g., interests, location, engagement level).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":22,"type":"strong"},{"start":36,"end":49,"type":"strong"},{"start":57,"end":87,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Track key metrics: Meltwater's solution provides all the tools you need to set yourself up for success and optimize your efforts. You can track open and conversion rates for each outreach campaign to gain insights into what’s truly resonating and having the most impact.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":18,"type":"strong"},{"start":138,"end":213,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ctable class=\"table-primary\"\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhat\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eHow\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWhy\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse Variation\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTest different subject lines, lengths, and pitch angles\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFind what gets the best response rate before scaling\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSegment Your Audience\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTag influencers by interests, location, or engagement level\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMore relevant messaging, better response rates\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTrack Key Metrics\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMonitor open and conversion rates per campaign\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKnow what's resonating and where to optimize\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/9e94620c-25d5-4373-b6e3-6e99a081791b_Meltwater+Influencer+Marketing+Screenshot+1.png?auto=compress,format","alt":"Meltwater Influencer Marketing Outreach Dashboard","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1600,"height":1000},"id":"Zdh0LhEAACoACrV0","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Simple Art of The Follow-up","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It might seem like a simple concept, but the art of following up is often forgotten. Remember that influencers lead busy lives. A well-timed nudge to bring your message back to the top of their inbox is sometimes all it takes to get a response. Of course, give influencers some breathing room before immediately following up.","spans":[{"start":130,"end":146,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Typically, waiting at least 2-3 days before following up ensures you don’t come off as too pushy and overbearing with your first impression.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":140,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The golden rule for how many follow-ups you should send is typically capped at two. Trust that if there is genuine interest, they will reply once their schedule allows. And if the interest is only one-sided, keep it at two to save yourself the heartache of being ghosted.","spans":[{"start":20,"end":82,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Ready To Scale Your Influencer Outreach?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Once you’ve tested and optimized your outreach, take a moment to applaud yourself for a job well done. With your outreach strategy in place, carry on full steam ahead to maximize the impact for your brand. And don’t be afraid to get some help and use tools like Meltwater's Influencer Marketing suite, especially as your outreach continues to expand.","spans":[{"start":262,"end":294,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/suite/influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When it comes to growth, if you are looking for ways to build up your pipeline and find even more authentic and enthusiastic influencers, explore our recruitment strategy tips.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":176,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":83,"end":136,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"XsLHIBEAAFzB8STY","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-find-influencers-for-your-brand-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2020-05-18T17:50:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T11:44:35+0000","uid":"finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","url":"/en/blog/finding-the-right-influencers-for-your-brand","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Building meaningful connections and expanding your influence network is an ongoing adventure, and with the right influencer marketing tools and strategies, the possibilities are endless!","spans":[{"start":113,"end":139,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y6xlSRIAACkAB7me","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-10-influencer-marketing-platforms-for-2026-compared","first_publication_date":"2023-01-11T23:49:09+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-05T12:14:45+0000","uid":"best-influencer-marketing-platforms","url":"/en/blog/best-influencer-marketing-platforms","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Don’t be intimidated by your influencer outreach, but do be intentional.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"strong"},{"start":54,"end":71,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Remember, there’s a human on the other side of your emails, so make sure your pitch stands out, is engaging, and is clear.","spans":[{"start":20,"end":58,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Test, optimize, and test.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"If you haven’t heard back, there’s no harm in a little nudge to show you are serious about a collaboration.","spans":[{"start":47,"end":61,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"And last but not least, don’t be afraid to use influencer management tools to help.","spans":[{"start":43,"end":82,"type":"strong"},{"start":47,"end":74,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Y6tKMhIAACwABiJP","type":"blog_post","tags":["ToFu","tools"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"top-influencer-management-platforms--tools-2026","first_publication_date":"2022-12-29T19:43:48+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T13:54:01+0000","uid":"influencer-management-platforms-tools","url":"/en/blog/influencer-management-platforms-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs about Influencer Outreach","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you write an influencer pitch that actually gets a response?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Whether that is a collaboration proposal, a product review, or an event invitation, a strong pitch is concise, personal, and clear about its purpose from the very first line. Addressing the influencer by name, referencing the type of content they create, and explaining why the partnership is a good fit shows you have done your research rather than blasting out a generic template. Reflecting your brand voice throughout also sets the right tone from the start.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Should you send individual emails or mass outreach when scaling influencer marketing?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Both have a place depending on the campaign. Individual emails work best for nuanced, highly personalized campaigns where the influencer relationship is central to the brief, while mass outreach is better suited to larger-scale campaigns where you need to move fast without losing a personal touch. Tools like Meltwater's email integration let you do both from a single platform using customizable templates and custom fields.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do you personalize influencer outreach at scale without it feeling generic?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Segmenting your influencer list by criteria such as interests, location, and engagement level lets you tailor your message to different groups rather than sending the same email to everyone. Customizable templates with dynamic fields let you inject personalized details into each message, even when sending in bulk. The goal is for every influencer to feel like the email was written specifically for them.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How many follow-up emails should you send to an influencer?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Two is the general rule. A well-timed follow-up sent 2 to 3 days after your initial email is often all it takes to bring your message back to the top of a busy inbox. Sending more than two follow-ups risks coming across as pushy, and if genuine interest is there, the influencer will respond when their schedule allows.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What metrics should you track when testing influencer outreach emails?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Open rates and conversion rates are the two most important metrics to watch, as they tell you whether your subject line is compelling enough to get the email opened and whether your pitch is strong enough to drive a response. Tracking these across different campaign versions and audience segments helps you identify the specific elements that are working rather than guessing.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Why is authenticity so important in influencer outreach?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Audiences can tell when influencer content feels transactional rather than genuine, which is why brands that invest in real, long-term relationships with creators tend to see stronger results. Outreach that feels personal and aligned with what an influencer already does builds the foundation for that kind of authentic partnership. A collaboration that feels forced from the first email is unlikely to produce content that resonates.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What is the biggest mistake brands make when scaling influencer outreach?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Pitching all their options at once without testing first is one of the costliest mistakes, as it leaves no room to learn what is working before the opportunity has passed. Starting with a few variations of your pitch across a smaller group lets you identify the approach with the best response rate before rolling it out more broadly. Scaling too fast without that optimization step often means scaling something that was not working in the first place.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Request a demo today to see how Meltwater Influencer Marketing can help amplify your influencer email outreach:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":111,"type":"strong"},{"start":85,"end":110,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"Zc4oOhIAACcA81_b","type":"blog_post","tags":["klear"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","first_publication_date":"2024-02-23T16:53:10+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T11:48:57+0000","uid":"how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","url":"/en/blog/how-to-approach-influencers-through-emails","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":true}},{"id":"ae-O9hIAACgAE6BD","uid":"generative-ai-trust-yougov","url":"/en/blog/generative-ai-trust-yougov","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22ae-O9hIAACgAE6BD%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2026-04-27T16:37:30+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-01T12:51:54+0000","slugs":["trust-in-the-age-of-ai-what-consumers-really-think-about-ai-generated-content"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"Trust in the Age of AI: What Consumers Really Think About AI Generated Content","published_date":"2026-04-27","author_name":"Lance Concannon","subtitle_tagline":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Download the full report","spans":[{"start":0,"end":24,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aeZx5RIAACgABfmL","type":"resource","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"public-perception-of-ai-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2026-04-20T23:31:53+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-03T14:50:38+0000","uid":"you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","url":"/resources/you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":0,"end":24,"type":"label","data":{"label":"cta-button"}}],"direction":"ltr"}],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"AI \u0026 Data Analytics"},{"category1":"PR \u0026 Communications"},{"category1":"Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afQ9HcBOoF08xh0V_TheTrustGap_WhatPublicConversationRevealsAboutReputationinAustralianInsurance.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=23,0,1875,1250\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"afQ9HcBOoF08xh0V","edit":{"x":23,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afQ9HcBOoF08xh0V_TheTrustGap_WhatPublicConversationRevealsAboutReputationinAustralianInsurance.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=335,0,1250,1250\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afQ9HcBOoF08xh0V","edit":{"x":335,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Generative AI is everywhere now. You see it in content, campaigns, customer experiences, and even internal workflows. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It promises speed, scale, and efficiency, but there’s a harder question sitting underneath all that progress; Do your audiences actually trust it?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That’s what we set out to understand in our latest report with YouGov, based on responses from nearly 10,000 people across seven global markets. The results point to something many brands are starting to feel in practice - AI is a double-edged sword, offering cost savings and productivity gains, but potentially damaging customer trust.","spans":[{"start":40,"end":69,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aeZx5RIAACgABfmL","type":"resource","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"public-perception-of-ai-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2026-04-20T23:31:53+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-03T14:50:38+0000","uid":"you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","url":"/resources/you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Contents","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Consumers Aren’t as Excited as You Might Think","spans":[{"start":0,"end":46,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Real Concern Isn’t AI, It’s What AI Enables","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Confidence Is High, but So Is Doubt","spans":[{"start":0,"end":35,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Context Shapes Acceptance More than Capability","spans":[{"start":0,"end":46,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"AI Can Cost You Trust if You’re Not Careful","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Transparency Is Now the Baseline","spans":[{"start":0,"end":32,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What This Means for You","spans":[{"start":0,"end":23,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Want the Full Picture?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":22,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQs: Trust in AI-Generated Content","spans":[{"start":0,"end":35,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Consumers Aren’t as Excited by Gen AI as You Might Think","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"There’s a gap between how the industry talks about AI and how people actually feel about it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You might assume that excitement is high because, after all, AI dominates headlines and product roadmaps for seemingly most companies. But when you look at consumer sentiment, the picture is more mixed. In fact, more people (51%) feel uncertain or sceptical than excited (39%) about a future shaped by generative AI.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That hesitation means your audience isn’t automatically on board with AI-driven content, no matter how advanced or efficient it is. By introducing a new tool into your workflow, you’re introducing a new layer of doubt into how your content is perceived.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Younger audiences are more open, with 48% of those aged 25-34 reporting that they are excited by AI, compared to only 31% of those aged over 55. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Some markets are more optimistic than others too; Germany (56%) and Singapore (55%) show the highest levels of excitement towards AI, while the UK (23%) and the U.S. (25%) are the least excited. But overall, enthusiasm is far from universal. You can’t assume broad AI acceptance, you have to earn it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Real Concern isn’t AI, it’s What AI Enables","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When people talk about AI risk, they’re rarely talking about the technology itself, they’re talking about outcomes.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Consumers are deeply concerned about how AI-generated content could be used. Fake news, scams, misleading information, and the erosion of authenticity all rank high on their list of worries. These aren’t fringe concerns; they’re held by a large majority of respondents across every market we studied.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That tells you something important: your audience is not evaluating AI in isolation, but judging it based on the potential for misuse.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"So when your brand uses AI, people don’t always see the efficiency and cost savings that drove you to adopt the technology; they see risk, and unless you address that risk head-on, it can quietly undermine your credibility.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Confidence is High, but so is Doubt","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Many consumers believe they can spot AI-generated content, but at the same time, an overwhelming majority worry that people in general won’t be able to tell what’s real and what isn’t.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That contradiction creates a strange dynamic in which, on a personal level, people feel confident, but on a societal level they feel uneasy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For brands, that means trust is fragile. Even if your content is accurate and well-intentioned, it exists in an environment where people expect confusion, misinformation, and blurred lines between human and machine.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For brands, the battle is more than fighting for consumer attention now, they’re competing to earn trust. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Context Shapes Acceptance More than Capability","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not all AI use is judged equally; the context of how and where it’s used matters when audiences form their opinions. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Consumers are more comfortable with AI in areas like entertainment and advertising, where 53% and 47% respectively said they found it acceptable, perhaps because these are spaces where creativity, experimentation, and even a bit of artificiality are expected, so the stakes feel lower.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But shift that same AI into news (21% acceptable), politics (18% acceptable), or other high-trust environments, and attitudes change quickly. Acceptance drops and resistance rises.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This is one of the clearest signals from the data; People don’t reject AI outright, instead they evaluate it based on context, intent, and perceived impact.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"So the question for your brand isn’t just “Should we use AI?” It’s “Where does AI make sense, and where does it damage trust?”","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"AI Can Cost You Trust if You’re Not Careful","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"One of the strongest findings in the report is the impact of AI on brand trust. A significant portion (32%) of consumers say they would trust a brand less if they knew its content was generated using AI, whereas only a small minority (15%) say it would increase their trust.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That imbalance should get your attention, since it means AI doesn’t come with a built-in trust advantage; rather, in many cases, it starts at a disadvantage.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It gets worse when certain lines are crossed, for example, if AI-generated content feels misleading, if it’s used without disclosure, or if it replaces human creativity entirely, trust drops sharply.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"So if you’re using AI, you need to think beyond efficiency and consider the impact on audience perception. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Transparency Is Now the Baseline","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If there’s one takeaway from the Trust in the Age of Generative AI report that you act on, make it this; Consumers expect you to be transparent about your use of AI, as a basic requirement.","spans":[{"start":29,"end":73,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aeZx5RIAACgABfmL","type":"resource","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"public-perception-of-ai-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2026-04-20T23:31:53+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-03T14:50:38+0000","uid":"you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","url":"/resources/you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":105,"end":189,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An overwhelming majority say it’s important that brands clearly disclose when content has been created using generative AI, and that expectation holds across every market in the study.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This is an interesting shift in how trust works, because in the past, brands could focus on outcomes, but now the process matters too. People want to know how content is made, not just what it says, and if they feel you’re hiding something, even unintentionally, it can erode trust quickly.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Transparency has become a signal of credibility.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What This Means for You","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI is not going away. You’re going to use it, your competitors are going to use it, and your audience knows that. The difference will come down to how you use it.","spans":[{"start":147,"end":150,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands that win in this next phase won’t be the ones that adopt AI the fastest, but the ones that use it thoughtfully, communicate it clearly, and keep human judgment at the centre of what they do.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"That means being deliberate about where AI adds value, setting clear guidelines for disclosure, and monitoring how your audience responds, not just assuming they’ll accept it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Most of all, it means recognising that trust is now part of your AI strategy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Want the Full Picture?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This is just a snapshot of what we found. The full report breaks down how attitudes differ by country, age group, and content type. It also explores where AI is gaining acceptance, where it’s facing resistance, and what that means for your communications strategy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If you’re thinking about how to use AI without damaging trust, it’s worth a closer look.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Download the full report to see the complete data and insights.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":63,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aeZx5RIAACgABfmL","type":"resource","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"public-perception-of-ai-generated-content","first_publication_date":"2026-04-20T23:31:53+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-03T14:50:38+0000","uid":"you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","url":"/resources/you-gov-public-perception-ai-generated-content","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":0,"end":63,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":63,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQs: Trust in AI-Generated Content","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Do people trust AI-generated content?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Most consumers are sceptical about AI-generated content. Over half feel uncertain, and many say it can reduce trust in brands using it.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Why don’t consumers trust AI content?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Trust issues stem from concerns about misinformation, fake news, scams, and lack of authenticity enabled by AI-generated content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Does AI-generated content hurt brand trust?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Yes, it can. Around one-third of consumers say they trust brands less when they learn content is AI-generated, especially without disclosure.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"When is AI-generated content acceptable?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Acceptance depends on context. Consumers are more comfortable with AI in marketing and entertainment, but less so in news, politics, or high-trust industries.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Can people detect AI-generated content?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Many believe they can identify AI content, but most worry that others cannot, contributing to broader uncertainty and distrust.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How important is AI transparency for brands?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Transparency is essential. Most consumers expect brands to clearly disclose when AI is used to create content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Should brands use AI in content creation?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Yes, but carefully. Brands should use AI where it adds value, maintain human oversight, and align usage with audience expectations.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How can brands build trust when using AI?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"To build trust, brands should disclose AI use, avoid misleading content, apply AI in appropriate contexts, and prioritise human input.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"aeXhFxIAACcABQwR","uid":"verified-youtube-data-in-meltwater","url":"/en/blog/verified-youtube-data-in-meltwater","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22aeXhFxIAACcABQwR%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":[],"first_publication_date":"2026-04-22T15:08:15+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-05T09:21:09+0000","slugs":["introducing-verified-youtube-data-in-meltwater-influencer-marketing"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"Introducing Verified YouTube Data in Meltwater Influencer Marketing","published_date":"2026-04-22","author_name":"Neil Brennan","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":"Company News","categories":[{"category1":"Influencer Marketing"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":"YouTube"}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afQ4HMBOoF08xhzr_Youtube-MeltwaterPartnerhsip.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=23,0,1875,1250\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"afQ4HMBOoF08xhzr","edit":{"x":23,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/afQ4HMBOoF08xhzr_Youtube-MeltwaterPartnerhsip.png?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=335,0,1250,1250\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"afQ4HMBOoF08xhzr","edit":{"x":335,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Influencer marketing is evolving, and so is the way brands measure success. That’s why Meltwater is proud to announce a new partnership with YouTube, integrating YouTube’s Creator Partnerships API directly into our Influencer Marketing platform.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As one of a select group of partners chosen by YouTube, this collaboration brings verified, first-party data into every stage of the influencer marketing workflow, marking a significant step forward for how brands approach YouTube as a marketing channel.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Contents","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Challenge: Growing Investment, Limited Visibility","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"A Strategic Shift for Influencer Marketing","spans":[{"start":0,"end":42,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"More Than Discovery: End-to-End Campaign Management","spans":[{"start":0,"end":51,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What This Means for Brands","spans":[{"start":0,"end":26,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why this Partnership Matters for Influencer Marketing","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQ: Meltwater Influencer Marketing \u0026 YouTube’s Creator Partnerships API","spans":[{"start":0,"end":72,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Challenge: Growing Investment, Limited Visibility","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"YouTube has become one of the most powerful platforms for creator-led marketing. With long-form content, deeper storytelling, and highly engaged audiences, it plays an increasingly central role in brand strategy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But as investment in YouTube grows, so do expectations. Marketers are facing a persistent challenge: public metrics only tell part of the story, making it difficult to fully understand true performance. At the same time, audience fit is hard to validate before campaigns even begin, and campaign impact is often fragmented or unclear once they are underway. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As a result, brands are investing more in YouTube, but without full confidence in performance or ROI.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The Solution: Verified Data, Built Into the Workflow","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Through this new partnership, Meltwater Influencer Marketing integrates verified, non-public YouTube data directly from creators in the YouTube Partner Program into influencer workflows, unlocking a new level of transparency and control.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But there’s more to this story than simply providing additional data for marketers - it’s about transforming how YouTube campaigns are planned, executed, and measured.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"With this integration, brands can:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Discover and evaluate creators using verified performance and audience insights","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Validate audience fit before investing in partnerships","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Track campaign performance at the video level","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Connect creator activity directly to measurable outcomes","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Optimize strategy and budget based on real performance data","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In short, YouTube becomes a measurable, optimizable channel, not a black box.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"A Strategic Shift for Influencer Marketing","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This partnership represents a broader shift in how YouTube is used within the marketing mix, empowering brands to move beyond discovery and treat YouTube as a true performance channel.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ctable\u003e\n  \u003cthead class=\"table-primary alternate-color\"\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBefore\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAfter\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/thead\u003e\n  \u003ctbody\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eYouTube was a partially visible channel\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eYouTube is a fully integrated, measurable channel\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eDecisions relied on surface-level metrics\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eDecisions are driven by verified, first-party data\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eReporting was fragmented\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eCampaign performance is clearly tracked end-to-end\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"More Than Discovery: End-to-End Campaign Management","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"While many platforms focus on helping brands find creators, Meltwater Influencer Marketing goes further. Our platform enables teams to:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Discover: Search and filter YouTube creators.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":10,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Vet: Evaluate engagement quality, audience demographics, and content performance using verified data.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":5,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Activate: Seamlessly manage campaigns and assign YouTube deliverables.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Measure: Track video-level performance and access unified campaign reporting.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This end-to-end approach ensures that every stage of a campaign is informed by reliable, actionable insights.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What This Means for Brands","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For marketers, this partnership unlocks an entirely new level of confidence in YouTube investments. Instead of relying on surface-level metrics or post-campaign assumptions, brands can now make decisions grounded in verified, first-party data at every stage of the campaign lifecycle.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"With deeper visibility into creator performance and audience quality, marketers can make smarter, more informed decisions about who they partner with, reducing risk and ensuring stronger alignment from the outset. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This means validating audience fit before committing budget, not after results come in.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"At the same time, campaign impact becomes far easier to prove. With access to more accurate, video-level performance data, brands can clearly connect creator activity to real outcomes, moving beyond vanity metrics to demonstrate true business value. This level of transparency not only strengthens reporting, but also builds internal confidence among stakeholders.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The ability to track performance more precisely also opens the door to ongoing optimization. So instead of waiting until a campaign ends to evaluate success, marketers can monitor results in real time and adjust strategy, creative direction, or budget allocation based on what’s actually working.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ultimately, this shifts influencer marketing on YouTube from an experimental or awareness-driven tactic into a measurable, performance-driven growth channel. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As the space continues to mature, this level of measurement is no longer a “nice to have” but a requirement for brands looking to scale their investments with confidence and accountability.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why this Partnership Matters for Influencer Marketing","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This isn’t just a new feature or data integration, it’s a shift in how YouTube fits into the broader influencer marketing ecosystem. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"With verified data embedded across discovery, vetting, activation, and measurement, Meltwater Influencer Marketing is helping brands turn YouTube into a fully measurable, performance-driven channel.\n","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQ: Meltwater Influencer Marketing \u0026 YouTube’s Creator Partnerships API ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What makes this YouTube partnership different from existing influencer marketing tools?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Unlike platforms that rely primarily on public data, Meltwater Influencer Marketing integrates verified, first-party data directly from YouTube. This provides a deeper, more accurate view of creator performance, audience quality, and campaign impact—enabling brands to make more confident, data-driven decisions across the entire campaign lifecycle.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Do brands need to take any additional steps to access YouTube data in their campaign?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Yes. To access verified campaign data, brands must connect their YouTube account in the platform and include YouTube deliverables within their campaigns. In addition, creators need to link their content through YouTube’s ecosystem. These steps ensure data accuracy and alignment with YouTube’s measurement framework.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How is verified YouTube data made available in Klear?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Verified data is available for creators who have opted into YouTube’s Partner Program. This ensures that audience and performance insights are based on real viewer behavior, providing a more accurate and reliable view of creator performance.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What kind of insights can brands expect from this integration?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Brands gain access to richer performance insights beyond surface-level metrics, including video-level performance data, deeper audience insights, and more reliable engagement signals. This allows teams to evaluate creators more effectively, measure campaign success with greater precision, and optimize performance in real time.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Is this partnership only relevant for large-scale influencer programs?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Not at all. While enterprise teams will benefit from advanced measurement and reporting, brands of all sizes can use these capabilities to improve decision-making, reduce wasted spend, and scale their influencer marketing efforts more effectively. Whether running a single campaign or a global program, having access to verified data leads to better outcomes.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"aeZP0hIAACkABcXc","uid":"coachella-2026-top-influencers-brands","url":"/en/blog/coachella-2026-top-influencers-brands","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22aeZP0hIAACkABcXc%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["Intelligence Hub"],"first_publication_date":"2026-04-21T17:40:06+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T07:16:31+0000","slugs":["the-top-branded-influencer-posts-of-coachella-2026"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"The Top Branded Influencer Posts of Coachella 2026","published_date":"2026-04-21","author_name":"Ann-Derrick Gaillot and Anna Amarotti","subtitle_tagline":[],"category":null,"categories":[{"category1":"Influencer Marketing"},{"category1":"Marketing"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":"TikTok"}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"Minimalist Meltwater visual emphasizing “2 billion reach of the top 1000 Coachella influencers.” A straw sunhat with round sunglasses rests beside a smartphone displaying a cheerful woman in festival attire, with a blurred ferris wheel in the background, reinforcing a warm, summery Coachella aesthetic.","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeelBcBOoF08xL12_Landing-Page-1920x1250-7-.jpg?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=23,0,1875,1250\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"aeelBcBOoF08xL12","edit":{"x":23,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":"Minimalist Meltwater visual emphasizing “2 billion reach of the top 1000 Coachella influencers.” A straw sunhat with round sunglasses rests beside a smartphone displaying a cheerful woman in festival attire, with a blurred ferris wheel in the background, reinforcing a warm, summery Coachella aesthetic.","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeelBcBOoF08xL12_Landing-Page-1920x1250-7-.jpg?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=335,0,1250,1250\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"aeelBcBOoF08xL12","edit":{"x":335,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Coachella has long been a proving ground for brands looking to drive visibility via influencer marketing, but in 2026, the festival’s content creator presence and performance seemed to reach new heights. Between superstar performances, we saw elaborate brand activations and countless influencer partnerships, giving far-flung audiences a lot to chew on throughout the two-weekend event. However, not every influencer post managed to break through the noise. Some drove major value for brands and audiences alike. Others cycled through the algorithm without making a mark. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"We used Meltwater’s AI-powered influencer marketing solution to see which content from 1,000 influential creators achieved the highest ROIs for their brand partners, including estimated media value (EMV). ","spans":[{"start":8,"end":60,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/influencer-marketing","target":"_self"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Note: EMV is a metric that quantifies the ROI of influencer marketing, showing what it would cost to achieve the same reach and engagement through paid media. It combines two components: 1) Benchmarks that show what each engagement action would cost in paid media across social channels, and 2) An earned multiplier that reflects the higher trust and impact of influencer content compared to traditional ads.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":408,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":4,"end":6,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The social media posts that stood out most were native to each creator’s style and engaged viewers with giveaways, tutorials, heartwarming moments, and more. As a whole, the resulting top 10 list offers a clear takeaway for marketers: With the right creator and the right moment to share, brands across sectors can derive meaningful value and impact from influencer partnerships pegged to major cultural events. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Read on for inspiration, tips, and takeaways that marketers can use as they plan for summer festival season, the World Cup, and beyond.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Methodology: The below list is based on Meltwater data analyzing Coachella posts from the top 1,000 content creators related to the festival across Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, Facebook, X, Twitch, and Pinterest from April 1 to April 20, 2026. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":243,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":11,"end":13,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeelBsBOoF08xL13_CoachellaInfluencersInfographic.png?auto=format,compress","alt":"Detailed Meltwater infographic titled “Coachella 2026: The Influencer Marketing Playbook.” It showcases top-performing brand partnerships like Revolve, Rhode, and Revive with metrics such as estimated media value and engagement rates, alongside insights highlighting TikTok’s dominance, early posting advantages, and the impact of educational content.","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":1672,"height":941},"id":"aeelBsBOoF08xL13","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Contents:","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The Top Branded Influencer Posts of Coachella 2026","spans":[{"start":0,"end":50,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":50,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"1. Nicole Sahebi for Revolve","spans":[{"start":0,"end":28,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":28,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"2. Deja Clark for Revice","spans":[{"start":0,"end":24,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":24,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"3. Kayla Ryan for Rhode","spans":[{"start":0,"end":23,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":23,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"4. Kayla Ryan for Kopari Beauty","spans":[{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"6. Priscila Escoto for Huda Beauty","spans":[{"start":0,"end":34,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":34,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"7. Tarayummy for JSX","spans":[{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":20,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"8. Natalie \u0026 the Aguilars for Barbie","spans":[{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"9. Tabitha Swatosh for The Kacey Welch Method","spans":[{"start":0,"end":45,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":45,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"10. Samantha Costantini for NYX","spans":[{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":31,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Honorable Mention: Matt Newman for Cécred and Arachne","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Key Takeaways: Influencer Partnerships for High-Profile Events","spans":[{"start":0,"end":62,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":62,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"FAQ: Influencer Marketing for High-Profile Events","spans":[{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}},{"start":0,"end":49,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"The Top Branded Influencer Posts of Coachella 2026","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"All 10 of the top-performing Coachella posts from influencers partnering with brands came from TikTok. This proportion contrasts with the makeup of overall posts from 1000 of some of the most influential content creators at the festival, of which 42% came from Instagram and 41% from TikTok. Here are the branded influencer posts that stood apart from the rest and what marketers can learn from each of them.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Nicole Sahebi for Revolve","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeeoV8BOoF08xL39_CoachellaPostRevolve.png?auto=format%2Ccompress\u0026w=501\u0026h=874","alt":"Video still of influencer @nicoleisgod at an outdoor Revolve event, browsing a clothing rack and holding up a white dress, with racks of colorful garments and greenery in the At the end of the TikTok video, she asks viewers to enter a giveaway for the free items by naming their favorite Coachella artist in the comments. That invitation to participate helped the above post collect more than 18,400 comments. Those, along with hundreds of thousands of shares and saves, helped this post generate nearly $25 million in EMV for Revolve. ","spans":[{"start":348,"end":436,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Steal this tactic: Using comments as entries gives followers a low-barrier way to participate in contests while drumming up engagement. Hailey Bieber’s cosmetics brand Rhode hosted a pop-up at the festival, which fans got to experience vicariously through beauty and lifestyle influencer Kayla Ryan’s video for Day Two of the festival. In the video, Kayla captures all the activation has to offer, including a look at specific Rhode products and even a special appearance from Mrs. Bieber herself. It may have been that celebrity appearance that helped this post punch above its weight with a nearly 70% engagement rate, the highest of any post in our top 10.","spans":[{"start":424,"end":585,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Like the previous post of hers, this one is a short and simple take on her signature get-ready-with-me (GRWM) posts showing off Revice items in a festival-ready outfit. Plus, this example also featured a celebrity appearance, though this one was digital. Deja’s TikTok uses the hashtag #laufey and audio from the song “Lover Girl” by Coachella performer Laufey, who then left a comment on the video that garnered over 83,100 likes of its own.","spans":[{"start":380,"end":567,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. She waits until the end of her GRWM video to highlight the brand tagged, using Kopari Beauty’s mineral SPF powder as the finishing touch on her look. As a result, the post avoids the kind of hard sell that social media users have grown tired of from content creators. It’s that kind of authenticity that helps Kayla’s sponsored posts resonate with her audience. In this one, Kayla’s soft sell approach drove about $5.4 million in EMV for the cosmetics brand.","spans":[{"start":273,"end":291,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/sunscreen-ai-visibility-index","target":"_blank"}},{"start":540,"end":636,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Steal this tactic: Sponsored influencer content resonates most when it is integrated seamlessly into a creator’s established style and digital language. Instead of providing word-for-word scripts or insisting on front-loading brand mentions, give content creators license to do what they do best: introducing your brand to an audience that already trusts them. Enlist influencers as creative partners, and watch that approach pay off in high ROI. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":447,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":19,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"6. For the above TikTok, the Aguilars visited the toy brand’s festival pop-up, which featured their and other select influencers’ portraits on the walls. That personal touch from Barbie translated into a moment of authentic appreciation for the partnerships it has forged with content creators. The 230+ comments left on the post featured many well-wishes and positive encouragement from fans sharing in the sisters’ excitement. Overall, the feel-good video resonated with the Aguilar family’s audience and paid off in over $3.2 million in EMV for the Barbie brand.","spans":[{"start":274,"end":414,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"9. Rate\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n\n    \u003cdiv style=\"flex:1;border:1px solid #eeeeee;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;text-align:center;\"\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:14px;font-weight:700;\"\u003e$2.4M\u003c/div\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:11px;color:#a3a3a3;\"\u003eEMV\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n\n  \u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Seeing a makeup look is one thing, but knowing how to recreate it is a whole other challenge for everyday makeup consumers. Beauty and lifestyle influencer Samantha Costantini demystified the process behind her Day Three makeup look using a full face of NYX products. The step-by-step video is over four minutes long, giving viewers a detailed explanation of how to incorporate 17 — yes, 17 — different NYX products into a single makeup routine. Featuring educational content that cosmetics enthusiasts will want to go back to, the post punched above its weight in saves, with over 6,100 users bookmarking it for later, and an engagement rate of over 16%.","spans":[{"start":83,"end":122,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/reddit-beauty-trends-early-2026#bold_blush%3a_here_to_stay%2c_but_where_do_you_put_it%3f","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Honorable Mention: Matt Newman for Cécred and Arachne","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"image","url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aeevmcBOoF08xL6g_Coachella11Cecred-Edited.png?auto=format%2Ccompress\u0026w=504\u0026h=873","alt":"A person with long dark hair wears a green bandana and adjusts it with both hands while standing indoors. They wear a light denim shirt and gold bracelets. Text on screen reads “the oldest trick in the book…” suggesting a simple festival hair hack.","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":504,"height":873},"id":"aeevmcBOoF08xL6g","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003cdiv  \n    \u003cdiv style=\"flex:1;border:1px solid #eeeeee;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;text-align:center;\"\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:14px;font-weight:700;\"\u003e12M\u003c/div\u003e\n
