style=\"font-size:11px;color:#a3a3a3;\"\u003eSaves\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n\n    \u003cdiv style=\"flex:1;border:1px solid #eeeeee;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;text-align:center;\"\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:14px;font-weight:700;\"\u003e54.51%\u003c/div\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:11px;color:#a3a3a3;\"\u003eEng. Rate\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n\n    \u003cdiv style=\"flex:1;border:1px solid #eeeeee;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;text-align:center;\"\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:14px;font-weight:700;\"\u003e$18.6M\u003c/div\u003e\n      \u003cdiv style=\"font-size:11px;color:#a3a3a3;\"\u003eEMV\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n\n  \u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Though it wasn’t sponsored, the above post by hairstylist Matt Newman drove major value for two tagged brands, Cécred and Arachne, while outpacing all of the above posts. Using a scarf from the former brand and hair clips from the latter, he shows off a “hack” for festival-goers that resonated widely. The short video generated more than 1.2 million likes, over 478,800 saves, and about $18.6 million in EMV, outperforming. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Of the many factors that converged to make this post a viral hit, one was the educational value. Matt taught his 3.2 million followers how to finally keep their fashionable bandannas from falling off their heads and created a high-performing video in the process. The lesson here: Content that offers tangible value, like technique explainers or DIY solutions to common problems, lends itself well to driving engagement actions like comments and saves.  ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Steal this tactic: Like Cécred and Arachne, your brand also has champions highlighting you on social media. Track your organic mentions across platforms like Instagram and TikTok to find them, and you may just discover your next influencer partnership. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":253,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":253,"type":"strong"},{"start":108,"end":178,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/social-listening","target":"_self"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Key Takeaways: Influencer Partnerships for High-Profile Events","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Our analysis of the top-performing influencer posts featuring brands from Coachella 2026 revealed these key takeaways and trends:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"TikTok is the #1 platform for influencer posts at live events like Coachella. All of the 10 best-performing influencer posts tagging brands came from the video platform, highlighting its strength for engaging audiences in near-real time and its necessity for creator partnerships for events.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":78,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Earlier content drives the most value. Another commonality of the top 10: Every post came from the first weekend in the festival, with most coming from the first couple of days. This seems to suggest that the earlier content appears, the better-poised it is to capture the initial excitement of multi-week events while not getting overshadowed by their grand finales.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":39,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Giving creators experiences to document makes for engaging content. From gifting suites and pop-ups to accommodations and transportation, activations that allow audiences to share in creator experiences drive standout results for brands.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":68,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The entertainment field is wide open for non-beauty/fashion (but still relevant) brands. Seven out of the ten high-performing brands we highlighted were in the beauty or fashion sectors. Brands outside of those realms with engaging, relevant experiences to offer can stand out with new twists on well-worn formats like GRWMs or something completely new.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":89,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"The most successful sponsored content feels natural to the influencer. Every post in the top 10 was authentic to the creator’s style and subject niche, making them resonate easily with their audiences. For brands, this highlights just how important influencer creative freedom and authenticity are to driving ROI.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":71,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Giving audiences tangible value makes for content that sticks. Tutorials, styling inspiration, and practical hacks are engagement boosters. High numbers of saves signal that audiences found your post especially useful, so much so that they want to revisit them again and again.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":63,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Build your own influencer program that gets top 10-list results. See how you can identify the right creators, streamline campaign execution, and measure real impact with Meltwater Influencer Marketing. Use the form below to request a demo and see first-hand how to drive measurable growth for your brand.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"FAQ: Influencer Marketing for High-Profile Events","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What makes influencer marketing effective at high-profile events like Coachella?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":80,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s analysis of Coachella 2026 shows that effectiveness comes down to authenticity and audience value. The top-performing posts were native to each creator’s style and offered something engaging, such as giveaways, tutorials, or behind-the-scenes experiences. This combination consistently drove high engagement and strong EMV (Estimated Media Value).","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Which social media platforms perform best for influencer campaigns during live events?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":86,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"While platform mix matters, Meltwater’s Coachella 2026 data highlights TikTok as the clear performance leader for live events. Even though Instagram accounted for a similar share of total influencer content, every top-performing branded post by engagement came from TikTok, demonstrating the platform’s strength for leveraging live events.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How should brands measure influencer marketing success at large-scale events?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":77,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"According to Meltwater, success should be measured using a combination of engagement metrics and EMV. By analyzing likes, comments, shares, and saves alongside estimated media value, brands can better understand which influencer partnerships are delivering meaningful ROI during tentpole events.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What types of influencer content work best during cultural moments?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":67,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s Coachella 2026 insights show that content offering tangible value—like GRWM videos, tutorials, and practical hacks—drives high engagement, especially saves and shares. Interactive content such as giveaways can also significantly boost performance.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"When is the best time to activate influencer campaigns around major events?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":75,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater data reveals that the highest-performing Coachella 2026 posts were published during the first weekend, particularly in the first two of the three days. Launching content early allows brands to capture peak audience attention before saturation sets in.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How important is creative freedom for influencers in brand partnerships?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":72,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s analysis underscores that creative freedom is essential. The most successful Coachella posts seamlessly integrated brand messaging into the influencer’s existing content style. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How can brands choose the right influencers for high-profile events?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":68,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Using tools like Meltwater Influencer Discovery, brands can evaluate creators based on audience demographics, engagement rates, and brand alignment. The Coachella 2026 data shows that campaigns perform best when there’s a strong match between the influencer’s audience and the brand’s target market. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How can brands create successful influencer marketing campaigns at other major events (e.g., festivals, sports, global moments)?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":128,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s Coachella 2026 analysis provides a repeatable framework: prioritize TikTok-first strategies, activate early, focus on authentic creator partnerships, and deliver audience value. Brands can apply these insights to events like the World Cup, fashion weeks, and beyond to drive measurable influencer marketing success.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"}],"renewing_publish_date":false}}],"total":101,"previous":null,"next":2},"highlights":[],"editorsPick":[{"id":"YE8dghEAACAA0n2B","uid":"top-social-media-monitoring-tools","url":"/en/blog/top-social-media-monitoring-tools","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22YE8dghEAACAA0n2B%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["editors_pick","tools"],"first_publication_date":"2021-06-07T12:47:21+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T05:30:41+0000","slugs":["the-top-22-social-media-monitoring-tools-2026"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[],"data":{"title":"The Top 22 Social Media Monitoring Tools 2026","top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"A seesaw with two orange balls balancing on one end of the seesaw. Illustration of two megaphones amplifying a press release on a podium for our blog with press release examples and a free press release template ","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/fc0e68c3-721c-4dc3-8ac8-dd3dbd57b46e_Blog.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=140,0,1220,813\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"Y3gzJREAACsALrHL","edit":{"x":-220,"y":0,"zoom":1.5744158029556274,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/fc0e68c3-721c-4dc3-8ac8-dd3dbd57b46e_Blog.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=343,0,813,813\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"Y3gzJREAACsALrHL","edit":{"x":-456,"y":0,"zoom":1.3284132480621338,"background":"transparent"}}},"renewing_publish_date":false}},{"id":"Xz5L-hIAACQAPlzi","uid":"social-media-image-sizes","url":"/en/blog/social-media-image-sizes","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22Xz5L-hIAACQAPlzi%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["ToFu","popular"],"first_publication_date":"2020-08-31T20:29:07+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-03-21T18:42:34+0000","slugs":["full-guide-to-social-media-image-sizes-for-2025"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[{"id":"X8jokRIAACQAk6b3","type":"blog_post","lang":"de","uid":"social-media-bildgroessen"},{"id":"YV9BiRMAACAALCBD","type":"blog_post","lang":"fr","uid":"taille-image-reseaux-sociaux"}],"data":{"title":"Full Guide to Social Media Image Sizes for 2025","top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"Three circular objects, increasing in size. Ultimate guide to social media image sizes ","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/38bc0873-10d8-43aa-b9bf-63b8284d1474_Hero-1920x1280.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=0,0,1920,1280\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"YNYY6BAAACUAEKhT","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/38bc0873-10d8-43aa-b9bf-63b8284d1474_Hero-1920x1280.png?auto=compress,format\u0026rect=320,0,1280,1280\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"YNYY6BAAACUAEKhT","edit":{"x":-270,"y":0,"zoom":0.84375,"background":"transparent"}}},"renewing_publish_date":false}}],"featured":{"id":"aR80qxEAACsAQc7f","uid":"what-is-generative-engine-optimization","url":"/en/blog/what-is-generative-engine-optimization","type":"blog_post","href":"https://meltwater.cdn.prismic.io/api/v2/documents/search?ref=agAvvxIAACoALG0R\u0026q=%5B%5B%3Ad+%3D+at%28document.id%2C+%22aR80qxEAACsAQc7f%22%29+%5D%5D","tags":["site","ToFu","pillar","featured"],"first_publication_date":"2025-11-20T23:28:51+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-10T06:57:30+0000","slugs":["what-is-generative-engine-optimization-geo--how-to-do-it"],"linked_documents":[],"lang":"en-us","alternate_languages":[{"id":"aUmUwREAACcAc4d5","type":"blog_post","lang":"fr","uid":"cest-quoi-le-geo"},{"id":"aUQN2BEAACgAajEw","type":"blog_post","lang":"de","uid":"generative-engine-optimization-geo"}],"data":{"title":"What is Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)? \u0026 How to Do It","published_date":"2025-11-20","author_name":"Lance Concannon","subtitle_tagline":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"Free Download: Brand AI Visibility Guide","spans":[{"start":0,"end":40,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"adkiJxEAACsAdXew","type":"resource","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"own-your-ai-visibility---shape-what-ai-says-about-your-brand","first_publication_date":"2026-04-14T09:20:12+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-01T10:42:37+0000","uid":"own-brand-visibility-ai","url":"/resources/own-brand-visibility-ai","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":0,"end":40,"type":"label","data":{"label":"cta-button"}}],"direction":"ltr"}],"category":"Marketing","categories":[{"category1":"Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)"}],"social_networks":[{"social_network":null}],"top_image":{"dimensions":{"width":1920,"height":1280},"alt":"illustration of a robot holding a magnifying glass next to letters GEO","copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aR-YMmGnmrmGqGLR_Landing-Page-1920x1200-what-is-generative-engine-optimization-GEO.jpg?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=262,0,3699,2466\u0026w=1920\u0026h=1280","id":"aR-YMmGnmrmGqGLR","edit":{"x":262,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"square":{"dimensions":{"width":1080,"height":1080},"alt":null,"copyright":null,"url":"https://images.prismic.io/meltwater/aR-YMmGnmrmGqGLR_Landing-Page-1920x1200-what-is-generative-engine-optimization-GEO.jpg?auto=format,compress\u0026rect=879,0,2466,2466\u0026w=1080\u0026h=1080","id":"aR-YMmGnmrmGqGLR","edit":{"x":879,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"}}},"body_content":[{"type":"paragraph","text":"As generative AI reshapes how people research, learn, and make decisions, a new discipline is emerging: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO). Just as SEO helped brands get discovered on Google, GEO ensures they appear accurately and positively within AI-powered platforms like ChatGPT, Gemini, and Copilot. With millions of consumers now turning to generative engines instead of search engines, GEO is fast becoming essential for marketing and PR professionals looking to influence how their brand shows up in this new discovery layer, where conversations, not clicks, determine visibility.","spans":[{"start":194,"end":271,"type":"strong"},{"start":461,"end":481,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/ai-for-influencer-marketing","target":"_blank"}},{"start":492,"end":534,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYN0exEAAC0AT3DL","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-driven-brand-discovery-a-game-changer-for-marketers","first_publication_date":"2026-02-04T23:50:52+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:26:42+0000","uid":"ai-brand-discovery","url":"/en/blog/ai-brand-discovery","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Contents","spans":[{"start":0,"end":8,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What is Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":45,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Why Should Marketing \u0026 Insights Teams Care About GEO?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":53,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Does GEO Work? Key Mechanisms \u0026 Signals","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"How Can You Get Started With GEO?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":33,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"6 Common GEO Mistakes to Avoid","spans":[{"start":0,"end":30,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"What's the Difference Between GEO and SEO?","spans":[{"start":0,"end":42,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Increase Your GEO Readiness with Meltwater","spans":[{"start":0,"end":42,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Generative Engine Optimization FAQ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":34,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Table-Of-Contents"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What is Generative Engine Optimization (GEO)?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is the practice of ensuring that AI platforms such as ChatGPT, Google Gemini, Llama, etc., provide accurate, positive, and up-to-date information about your brand when users ask questions. These platforms are referred to as Generative Engines, Answer Engines, or Large Language Models (LLMs). They are increasingly used by consumers to research purchases and make decisions.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"strong"},{"start":339,"end":391,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.mckinsey.com/capabilities/growth-marketing-and-sales/our-insights/new-front-door-to-the-internet-winning-in-the-age-of-ai-search","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"GEO is a way of making sure that AI platforms give people the right information about a company’s brand and products. ","spans":[{"start":58,"end":79,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For example, if a person asks ChatGPT: “What is a good family car for camping trips?” then the auto manufacturers would want ChatGPT to suggest their SUV models, and list the benefits. To make sure that happens, the auto brands need to use GEO.","spans":[{"start":39,"end":85,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"GEO ensures your brand shows up correctly within these AI-generated responses.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":78,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The practice of GEO involves several steps, but it is mostly focused on creating, structuring, and publishing content in the best way to influence the responses given by AI Generative Engines. ","spans":[{"start":72,"end":117,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In this article we’ll go into the detail of how GEO works, why it’s important, and how you can use it to make sure your brand is featured positively in AI platforms.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Sometimes, generative engine optimization (GEO) is referred to as answer engine optimization (AEO). Learn more about the differences and similarities of GEO and AEO, and take a look at the best AEO tools.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":204,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":66,"end":98,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYnKBxEAACgAonTS","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"what-is-answer-engine-optimization-aeo-the-complete-guide","first_publication_date":"2026-02-09T12:09:10+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-23T09:42:27+0000","uid":"aeo","url":"/en/blog/aeo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":100,"end":164,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTxcWhEAACkAXmsK","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"geo-vs-aeo-for-marketing-insights-when--how-to-use","first_publication_date":"2025-12-29T18:48:02+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:23:34+0000","uid":"geo-vs-aeo","url":"/en/blog/geo-vs-aeo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":170,"end":203,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYyuZhEAACwASrtF","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-top-5-answer-engine-optimization-aeo-tools-to-transform-your-content-strategy-in-2026","first_publication_date":"2026-02-19T18:29:37+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-21T12:52:33+0000","uid":"best-answer-engine-optimization-tools","url":"/en/blog/best-answer-engine-optimization-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Why Should Marketing \u0026 Insights Teams Care About GEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For a long time marketers have been able to rely on the fact that consumers almost exclusively use Google to research buying decisions. So they have been able to use Search Engine Optimization (SEO) to ensure their brand’s website appears in the organic search results, or use paid search advertising to buy ads on relevant search results. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"This is a highly effective way of targeting the right audience at the right time. But now things are changing.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"LLMs aren't just another channel. They sit on top of this ecosystem. They synthesize everything into a single conversation.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":123,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Allie Konchar, Partner \u0026 Chief Client Officer, Omniscient","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://beomniscient.com/blog/author/alliedecker/","target":"_blank"}},{"start":0,"end":57,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Author-Quote"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Changing user behavior: AI + conversational search","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Increasingly, consumers are using tools like ChatGPT to research their buying decisions. Data from Meltwater’s 2026 Global Digital Report shows that ChatGPT.com is now the fifth most visited website on the web.","spans":[{"start":89,"end":210,"type":"strong"},{"start":99,"end":137,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/global-digital-trends","target":"_blank"}},{"start":149,"end":160,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://chatgpt.com","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"While search engines remain popular, many search queries now produce an AI generated summary that often provide enough information to divert users away from clicking through to websites, because their question has already been answered. McKinsey reports that around half of all Google search queries already produce AI generated summaries, and that number is increasing all the time. ","spans":[{"start":237,"end":253,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.mckinsey.com/capabilities/growth-marketing-and-sales/our-insights/new-front-door-to-the-internet-winning-in-the-age-of-ai-search","target":"_blank"}},{"start":259,"end":338,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"With conventional web search, consumers are performing research manually, trying to find trustworthy sources and sifting through information to reach the answers they seek. But with Generative Engines, the AI is performing the research automatically, using its existing base knowledge alongside supplemental information pulled from more recent online sources. A search engine shows users where to find answers, while a Generative Engine just gives them the answer. ","spans":[{"start":30,"end":72,"type":"strong"},{"start":206,"end":249,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"For marketers, the key takeaway is that few top-of-funnel consumers are visiting your website, because during the awareness phase their information needs are being met by Generative Engines. This makes it more difficult to move them down into the consideration phase. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":13,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/ai-in-marketing-examples","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"TIP: For more data around growing AI usage and online consumer behavior, download our 2026 Global Digital Report, featuring nearly 700 pages statistics to help inform your GEO strategies next year.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":197,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":4,"type":"strong"},{"start":86,"end":112,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aO6eKhEAACkAz8WU","type":"blog_post","tags":["pinned resource"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"digital-2026-global-overview-report","first_publication_date":"2025-10-15T04:25:21+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T12:33:09+0000","uid":"digital-2026","url":"/en/blog/digital-2026","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":172,"end":186,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEETREAACoAR_X8","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"effective-geo-strategies-that-drive-llm-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-22T19:49:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:32:53+0000","uid":"geo-strategies","url":"/en/blog/geo-strategies","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Risks of ignoring GEO","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You already know that everybody is talking about AI, and all of your competitors are having the same discussions as you about what it means for their marketing strategy. Those who move decisively to implement GEO, ensuring their brands are represented clearly and positively in Generative Engines, will capture the largest share of those consumers who start their buying journey with an AI query. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Those who ignore GEO will lose ground, as growing numbers of consumers turn from search engines to Generative Engines, the size of the audiences that can be reached through conventional search marketing will decrease. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Does GEO Work? Key Mechanisms \u0026 Signals","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How do generative engines (LLMs) surface content?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s important to understand that while Generative Engines are based on similar technology, they all work a little differently. Broadly speaking they all use two different sources of knowledge to provide an answer to a query: ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ctable class=\"table-primary alternate-color\"\u003e\n  \u003ccaption\u003eHow Generative Engines (LLMs) Surface Content\u003c/caption\u003e\n  \u003cthead\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eGenerative Engine\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003ePrimary Retrieval Method\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eSources Most Often Cited\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eSources Less Likely to Be Cited\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eNotes on Behavior\u003c/th\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/thead\u003e\n  \u003ctbody\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eChatGPT\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eBing search + internal training data\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eReuters, AP News, Wikipedia, Encyclopedic sources\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eSocial media platforms (X/Twitter, Instagram)\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003ePrefers authoritative, neutral, structured sources with high trust signals.\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eGoogle Gemini\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eGoogle Search + YouTube + Knowledge Graph\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eReddit, YouTube, Google-indexed blogs\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eX/Twitter\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eStrongly favors user-generated content, community threads, and Google-owned properties.\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003ePerplexity\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eReal-time web crawling + citation-first retrieval\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eNews publishers, reputable blogs, research papers\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eLow-trust UGC\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eMost transparent engine — always cites sources and updates in real time.\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eClaude\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eInternal training data + selected retrieval\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eAcademic sources, reputable news, long-form articles\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eShort-form social media\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003ePrefers long-form text and well-structured explanatory content.\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Training Data: All LLMs begin by being trained on a huge volume of existing content and data — we’re talking about dozens of terabytes (a terabyte is 1,000Gb). This training data comes from a wide variety of sources (including your website), and gives the LLM its base knowledge about the world. The LLM does not have any knowledge of anything that happened after its initial training. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":15,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Live Web Search: To overcome the knowledge cut-off problem, the training data is supplemented with fresh information gathered from the internet. If the Generative Engine thinks that a user’s query would benefit from more up to date information than it already has, it fetches new content from trusted online sources and works that into its response. Google uses its own search engine to find fresh information, while ChatGPT uses various sources, including Microsoft Bing.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"It’s also important to note that AI does not quote any of this content verbatim in its responses. Much like a human, it uses the information it has learned to inform its own answer, and that can include information from various sources, including the press, competitors, consumer reviews. So don’t expect it to always be on-message!","spans":[{"start":63,"end":71,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/ai-generated-content","target":"_blank"}},{"start":321,"end":332,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Another key point is that unlike a search engine, an LLM does not have an index of content which it searches to find answers. It would be impossible to search ChatGPT for all mentions of your brand — that’s simply not how it works. ","spans":[{"start":159,"end":197,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYN5ChEAACwAT3jp","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"monitoring-brand-mentions-in-chatgpt-the-what-why-and-how","first_publication_date":"2026-02-06T17:18:27+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T07:12:23+0000","uid":"chatgpt-brand-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/chatgpt-brand-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An LLM is more similar to a human brain, in that it has a lot of knowledge stored in patterns and relationships between words and concepts. It is extremely difficult, verging on impossible, to examine and understand the detail of an LLM’s learned knowledge because of the sheer scale and complexity; this is a challenge referred to as Black Box AI. ","spans":[{"start":335,"end":347,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.ibm.com/think/topics/black-box-ai","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"But we do understand how Generative Engines work at a high level, and we can analyze the kind of responses they produce for relevant queries, which gives us insight into how we should optimize our content for increased visibility.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What content features improve GEO-visibility?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Bear in mind that when you’re optimizing content for Generative Engines, the goal is to write copy that is easier for AI to digest and incorporate into its responses.","spans":[{"start":107,"end":130,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Clarity: Avoid overly elaborate language. Do not use corporate-jargon. Keep it simple, professional, and to the point. Leave no room for ambiguity. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Authority: Wherever possible back up your statements with data from reputable sources, quotes from experts or industry leaders, and reference-able proof points. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":11,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Structure: Organize your pages well with clear titles, headings, and subheadings. Use heading tags (H1, H2, etc) correctly to specify the hierarchy of your content. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":11,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Recency: AI platforms look for more recent information from trusted sources to supplement their base knowledge, so in both owned and earned media, aim to publish fresh content recently.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":9,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In many respects GEO shares principles with good SEO practice. If you write consistently high quality content which addresses the needs of your target audience, and structure it correctly on well designed, standards compliant web pages, you will succeed.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":62,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"How Can You Get Started With GEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Benchmark current GEO performance ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"First you need to understand your starting point. Meltwater’s GenAI Lens is a tool that gives brands insight into how they are discussed by all major Generative Engines, including ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Perplexity, Grok, and Deepseek. ","spans":[{"start":50,"end":72,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/announcing-genai-lens-llm-monitoring","target":"_blank"}},{"start":180,"end":187,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYN5ChEAACwAT3jp","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"monitoring-brand-mentions-in-chatgpt-the-what-why-and-how","first_publication_date":"2026-02-06T17:18:27+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T07:12:23+0000","uid":"chatgpt-brand-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/chatgpt-brand-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":189,"end":195,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"acRQGBEAAC0ARbxg","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-track-brand-mentions-in-claude-ai","first_publication_date":"2026-03-26T19:38:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-30T11:19:36+0000","uid":"track-brand-mentions-claude","url":"/en/blog/track-brand-mentions-claude","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":197,"end":203,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"acQkFBEAACoARXsu","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"gemini-ai-brand-mention-tracking-strategies--tools","first_publication_date":"2026-03-25T18:18:11+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:28:38+0000","uid":"gemini-ai-brand-mention-tracking","url":"/en/blog/gemini-ai-brand-mention-tracking","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"With GenAI Lens you’ll be able to understand how your brand is already featured in LLMs, what source websites are used in those responses, and whether outdated or inaccurate information is being used. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Audit online footprint","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"We know that LLMs pull content from the web, which they use to inform their responses. So whatever is said about your brand online will significantly affect how Generative Engines talk about your company. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Start with your own website, this is the first place LLMs will look for information about your organization. But also focus on trusted, high authority third party sites like the mainstream news and specialist media within your industry, because these are the kinds of sources that LLMs will use. GenAI Lens can show you which websites the leading LLMS use to source information about your brand. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Once you have a comprehensive audit of how your brand is currently portrayed online, you can perform a gap analysis against your desired messaging, and build a plan for how to reach that objective.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: AI brand monitoring, AI media monitoring, and LLM monitoring will help you understand your online footprint. Learn how to track your LLM visibility and how to track LLM brand mentions.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":189,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":24,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTKq0RIAAC0AgXkV","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-build-an-ai-brand-monitoring-strategy","first_publication_date":"2025-12-05T15:36:36+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-05T11:33:42+0000","uid":"ai-brand-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/ai-brand-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":26,"end":45,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aS3WtxIAACcAedro","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-media-monitoring-definition-tools--strategy","first_publication_date":"2025-12-16T23:50:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-12-17T00:05:14+0000","uid":"media-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/media-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":51,"end":65,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aZ7KKBEAAC0AtF_V","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"prove-ai-impact-with-llm-monitoring-a-guide-for-marketing-teams","first_publication_date":"2026-02-25T10:20:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-25T17:19:39+0000","uid":"llm-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/llm-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":114,"end":152,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTaloxEAACwAVWfS","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-track-llm-visibility-metrics-tools--benefits","first_publication_date":"2025-12-10T18:50:23+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-29T22:51:16+0000","uid":"how-to-track-llm-visibility","url":"/en/blog/how-to-track-llm-visibility","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":157,"end":188,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEZ7REAACkASBji","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-brand-mentions-how-to-track-key-metrics--more","first_publication_date":"2026-01-23T00:38:25+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-29T22:51:39+0000","uid":"how-to-track-llm-brand-mentions","url":"/en/blog/how-to-track-llm-brand-mentions","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Map audience queries","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"An important difference between GEO and SEO is understanding how people search differently when using Generative Engines. Users interact conversationally with AI, and are likely to ask more complex questions in natural language, compared to simple keywords or short queries in a search engine. So it’s useful to get an idea of what kind of questions people will ask about your brand or industry, to help plan your GEO content.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about AI visibility for retail brands.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":54,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":22,"end":53,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"acGfbxEAACkAQTo4","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-visibility-for-retail-brands-what-it-is--how-to-win","first_publication_date":"2026-03-26T00:41:06+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:35:40+0000","uid":"ai-visibility-retail-brands","url":"/en/blog/ai-visibility-retail-brands","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Unlike search engines, at present the leading Generative Engines do not share data on popular user queries, so it’s not easy to find out exactly what people are asking about your business. But you can look at popular keyword searches associated with your brand, and brainstorm the most likely questions people will ask Generative Engines. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You can also use a social listening platform, like Meltwater Explore+ to analyze social media conversations around your brand and industry, to gain insight into common questions. Once you have mapped out all of the most likely questions people might ask, you can start creating content that answers those questions.","spans":[{"start":51,"end":69,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/capabilities/social-listening","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn how to connect social listening with generative engine optimization. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":79,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":78,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aZ2aFREAACkAsotB","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-connect-social-listening-with-generative-engine-optimization","first_publication_date":"2026-02-24T12:43:33+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T12:17:07+0000","uid":"social-listening-geo","url":"/en/blog/social-listening-geo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading4","text":"Align content to intent, not keywords","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Conventional SEO was built around search queries but GEO requires thinking in concepts, context, and authority — you need to focus on the intent of the query, rather than simply trying to match keywords. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"LLMs prioritize sources that provide comprehensive, well-structured, and contextually rich content. Focus your content strategy on:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Educational depth: Long-form resources that answer questions end-to-end. Publish explainers, FAQs, and data-backed insights that answer “how” and “why.”","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Credibility signals: Citations, quotes, and data from reputable sources (analyst studies, thought leadership, or trusted media).","spans":[{"start":0,"end":19,"type":"strong"},{"start":90,"end":108,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aamfRhEAACoAQWwv","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-llm-revolution-redefining-thought-leadership-with-pr","first_publication_date":"2026-03-05T15:27:22+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-06T08:57:56+0000","uid":"llm-thought-leadership","url":"/en/blog/llm-thought-leadership","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Rich media: Generative Engines pull information from videos and images as well as text content, so make use of varied content formats. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":12,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Consistency across channels: LLMs learn from media, social, and owned content together; ensure your narratives are aligned across them.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":27,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The key thing is to ensure that your content is genuinely helpful in answering those questions you mapped out. If your content is too focused on sales messages, it’s less likely to be used by the LLMs.","spans":[{"start":20,"end":44,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/ai-generated-content-examples","target":"_blank"}},{"start":48,"end":65,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"TIP: Discover innovative AI-generated content examples to enhance your digital strategy.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":88,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":0,"end":4,"type":"strong"},{"start":5,"end":87,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/ai-generated-content-examples","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"PR is essential to GEO","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"You can optimize the content published on your website and owned channels, but LLMs also source information from trusted third party websites. That means PR has a key role to play in ensuring your brand is featured in high authority earned media, including news features, expert comment from your spokespeople, product reviews, and any other kind of brand story traditionally generated through a media relations campaign.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about the importance of earned media for GEO and about PR Pitch GEO optimization.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":97,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":60,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTC_JRIAACsAfnX6","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"the-importance-of-earned-media-for-geo","first_publication_date":"2025-12-03T23:07:30+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T12:39:18+0000","uid":"earned-media-geo","url":"/en/blog/earned-media-geo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":71,"end":96,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aWbBYREAACgAysFn","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"pr-pitch-geo-optimization-how-pr-professionals-should-craft-pitches-for-ai-search-and-llm-responses","first_publication_date":"2026-01-14T20:21:56+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-03T12:17:32+0000","uid":"pr-pitch-geo-optimization","url":"/en/blog/pr-pitch-geo-optimization","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The tricky part is that unlike advertising, which can tell exciting and attention grabbing stories, PR professionals are limited to working only with the facts. But while you cannot always control the outcome of media outreach activity, ensuring that PR and GEO are aligned will help increase the volume of third party sources that feature the right messaging.","spans":[{"start":31,"end":42,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/generative-ai-for-ads","target":"_blank"}},{"start":251,"end":273,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aWaXxBEAACwAyoNc","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"pr--geo-how-to-win-visibility-in-ai-search--llm-answers","first_publication_date":"2026-01-13T21:55:58+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:32:05+0000","uid":"geo-pr","url":"/en/blog/geo-pr","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn how GEO is reshaping PR strategies.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":46,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":45,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abrsthEAACkANweB","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"narrative-control-in-2026-pr-strategies-for-ai--geo","first_publication_date":"2026-03-18T18:24:18+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T12:04:54+0000","uid":"pr-strategies-ai-geo","url":"/en/blog/pr-strategies-ai-geo","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"Measure, iterate, improve","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Meltwater’s GenAI Lens gives you the ability to track your GEO performance over time, so you can understand whether your strategy is working well and spot areas where you need to improve. The tool provides visualizations that show brand sentiment, emotion, key phrases, people, products and things mentioned, alongside citations.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":22,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Armed with this information you can identify gaps in your GEO strategy where you might need to create more or better content. You can also discover which third party sources have the greatest impact on your GEO performance, and use that information to optimize PR outreach to focus on the most important media. ","spans":[{"start":58,"end":70,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEETREAACoAR_X8","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"effective-geo-strategies-that-drive-llm-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-22T19:49:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:32:53+0000","uid":"geo-strategies","url":"/en/blog/geo-strategies","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Like SEO, this is a process of constant iteration and improvement. GEO should not be seen as a one-off project that will generate positive results in perpetuity, but rather an ongoing activity that needs to be integrated with the broader marketing mix.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn How to Measure PR Performance Across LLMs and Generative Search, and understand how to report on LLM visibility.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":123,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":74,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXkCBBEAACoAU_4c","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-measure-pr-performance-across-llms-and-generative-search","first_publication_date":"2026-01-27T19:06:52+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:28:21+0000","uid":"measure-pr-performance-llms","url":"/en/blog/measure-pr-performance-llms","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":80,"end":122,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYtW5xEAACkApO4i","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-report-on-llm-visibility-what-to-measure--why","first_publication_date":"2026-02-10T21:53:51+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-02-25T23:39:19+0000","uid":"how-to-report-on-llm-visibility","url":"/en/blog/how-to-report-on-llm-visibility","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Which Media Sources to Prioritize for GEO","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"When an LLM processes a user query, it can often detect that the response might require more recent information than it has access to in its original training data. In that situation it will look online for more up to date content that will help it to form a more accurate answer, but where exactly do Generative Engines look for this information?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"At Meltwater we used GenAI Lens to research which content sources the leading AI platforms were most likely to reference in their responses, and there were distinct differences between them all:","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Open AI ChatGPT: Heavily favors text-based news and newswires (Reuters, AP, PR Newswire) and reference sites (Wikipedia). Much less likely to cite social media and UGC. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":17,"type":"strong"},{"start":142,"end":159,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abmMTxEAACcANOxs","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-social-media-impacts-ai-search-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-03-23T19:43:11+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:38:14+0000","uid":"social-media-impact-ai-visibility","url":"/en/blog/social-media-impact-ai-visibility","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Google Gemini: Leans towards high quality UGC from sites like Reddit and YouTube, as well as other online sources within the Google ecosystem. Does not index X/Twitter content. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":14,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"Google Search AI Overviews: Highly focused on UGC from Reddit and YouTube, as well as Facebook, Yahoo and LinkedIn. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":28,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"list-item","text":"xAI Grok: Strongly skewed towards conversational UGC from X and Reddit. Less likely to cite traditional reference sources like Wikipedia, YouTube or academic sites. ","spans":[{"start":0,"end":10,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Unlike SEO, where most practitioners were happy to optimize only for Google, in GEO we have to consider the nuances of several different platforms; at the very least ChatGPT and Google AI Overviews. In short, it’s good practice to ensure you have up to date content propagated across a wide range of platforms where LLMs might look for supplemental information.  ","spans":[{"start":178,"end":197,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abmYoxEAACoANQCf","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"tracking-google-ai-overview-performance-brand-monitoring-guide","first_publication_date":"2026-03-17T18:09:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:42:48+0000","uid":"google-ai-overview-tracking","url":"/en/blog/google-ai-overview-tracking","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"In terms of which online sources are most likely to be referenced overall, we looked at several categories and charted the most frequently cited 10.1%\u003c/li\u003e\n\t\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"6 Common GEO Mistakes to Avoid","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Treating GEO like traditional SEO","spans":[{"start":0,"end":36,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Many teams focus on keywords, backlinks, and meta tags, but LLMs prioritize context, authority, and relevance. GEO requires content that answers full questions with clear, verifiable information, not keyword stuffing or link chasing.","spans":[{"start":76,"end":83,"type":"em"},{"start":85,"end":94,"type":"em"},{"start":100,"end":109,"type":"em"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. Ignoring source credibility and transparency","spans":[{"start":0,"end":47,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"AI models reward trustworthy, well-cited content. Publishing unverified claims, outdated statistics, or missing citations can lower visibility or lead to inaccurate AI summaries of your brand. Always include reputable data sources, authorship, and dates.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Failing to monitor how your brand appears in LLMs","spans":[{"start":0,"end":52,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Without ongoing media monitoring, brands miss when GenAI models misrepresent their products or replicate misinformation. LLM tracking tools like Meltwater’s GenAI Lens can surface these blind spots and help correct or update narratives quickly.","spans":[{"start":16,"end":32,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aS3WtxIAACcAedro","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-media-monitoring-definition-tools--strategy","first_publication_date":"2025-12-16T23:50:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2025-12-17T00:05:14+0000","uid":"media-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/media-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":121,"end":139,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aR98BhEAACoAQjsq","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"best-llm-tracking-tools-for-marketing-teams-2026-guide","first_publication_date":"2025-11-26T22:27:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-21T12:50:29+0000","uid":"llm-tracking-tools","url":"/en/blog/llm-tracking-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":145,"end":167,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}},{"start":145,"end":167,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Producing siloed content across channels","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If your website, press releases, and social media say different things, LLMs may produce inconsistent brand stories. GEO demands narrative alignment across earned, owned, and shared channels to reinforce message accuracy.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about AI press release optimization, about brand safety in AI, and about the impact of social media on AI visibility.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":133,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":51,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTMBAhEAACoAUD3q","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-press-release-optimization-how-to-optimize-press-releases-for-geo","first_publication_date":"2025-12-05T20:24:07+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-01-15T12:53:19+0000","uid":"ai-press-release-optimization","url":"/en/blog/ai-press-release-optimization","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":59,"end":77,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXOuqBEAACcATCXX","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"brand-safety-in-the-ai-era-how-to-correct-misinformation-in-chatbots-and-ai-search","first_publication_date":"2026-01-23T17:36:32+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T07:20:33+0000","uid":"brand-safety-ai","url":"/en/blog/brand-safety-ai","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":89,"end":132,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abmMTxEAACcANOxs","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-social-media-impacts-ai-search-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-03-23T19:43:11+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:38:14+0000","uid":"social-media-impact-ai-visibility","url":"/en/blog/social-media-impact-ai-visibility","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. Neglecting freshness and structured data","spans":[{"start":0,"end":43,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Generative Engines favor current, clearly organized information. Outdated pages or unclear formatting make it harder for AI to interpret your content correctly. Keep publishing new, well-structured insights tied to recent data or trends.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"6. Measuring the wrong success indicators","spans":[{"start":0,"end":41,"type":"strong"}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Traditional SEO KPIs like click-through rates or page rank don’t tell the full story. GEO should be measured by AI visibility share, narrative accuracy, sentiment, and engagement outcomes rather than traffic alone.","spans":[{"start":112,"end":125,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aUFRUhEAAC4AZbHO","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-visibility-how-to-track-your-brand-across-generative-ai","first_publication_date":"2025-12-16T13:36:54+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-05T11:46:08+0000","uid":"ai-visibility","url":"/en/blog/ai-visibility","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Avoiding these pitfalls ensures your GEO strategy builds trust, visibility, and consistency in how AI engines, and their billions of users, perceive your brand.","spans":[{"start":37,"end":49,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEETREAACoAR_X8","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"effective-geo-strategies-that-drive-llm-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-22T19:49:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:32:53+0000","uid":"geo-strategies","url":"/en/blog/geo-strategies","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn more about LLM sentiment analysis and take a look at LLM metrics and KPIs.","spans":[{"start":0,"end":85,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":5,"end":44,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aTxTDBEAACgAXlwR","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-sentiment-analysis-complete-guide--implementation","first_publication_date":"2025-12-29T18:58:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-01-09T08:19:40+0000","uid":"llm-sentiment-analysis","url":"/en/blog/llm-sentiment-analysis","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":64,"end":84,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aVQEkBEAACkAgniH","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"llm-metrics--kpis-how-to-measure--improve-ai-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-08T20:07:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:37:19+0000","uid":"llm-metrics-and-kpis","url":"/en/blog/llm-metrics-and-kpis","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"What's the Difference Between GEO and SEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Search Engine Optimization (SEO) is a marketing discipline that has existed almost for as long as search engines themselves. The goal of SEO is to ensure that when a consumer searches for a topic relevant to your business, an appropriate page from your brand’s website is listed as close to the top of the search results as possible. SEO involves creating the right kind of content for your website, and ensuring that it is correctly structured, as well as encouraging high quality third-party websites to link to your own.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"preformatted","text":"\u003ctable class=\"table-primary alternate-color\"\u003e\n  \u003ccaption\u003eComparison of Traditional SEO and LLM Optimization (GEO)\u003c/caption\u003e\n  \u003cthead\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eFactor\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eTraditional SEO\u003c/th\u003e\n      \u003cth\u003eLLM Optimization (GEO)\u003c/th\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/thead\u003e\n  \u003ctbody\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003ePrimary Goal\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eRank webpages in Google search results\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eAppear in AI-generated answers and summaries\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eOptimization Focus\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eKeywords, backlinks, technical SEO\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eClear entities, structured content, narrative consistency\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eHow Users Discover Content\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eSearch queries → SERPs → click-through\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003ePrompts → AI answers → source citations or summaries\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eContent Format Priorities\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eLong-form content optimized for keywords\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eExtractable blocks: bullets, tables, FAQs, definitions\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eHow Engines Retrieve Data\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eWeb crawling \u0026amp; indexing\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eLive web search + training data + citation patterns\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eWhat Engines Prefer to Cite\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eHigh-authority sites with strong SEO signals\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eAuthoritative, structured, fact-rich sources\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eSuccess Indicators\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eRankings, impressions, organic traffic\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eAI citations, inclusion in summaries, answer recall\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eUpdate Frequency Needed\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eModerate (when rankings drop)\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eHigh — LLMs prefer fresh, date-stamped information\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eBest Use Cases\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eDriving organic search traffic\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eShaping AI answers, reputation correction, brand visibility\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eBiggest Advantage\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eStable long-term traffic\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eAbility to influence narratives inside AI platforms\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n    \u003ctr\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eBiggest Challenge\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eRequires extensive link-building\u003c/td\u003e\n      \u003ctd\u003eRequires constant monitoring \u0026amp; multi-platform visibility\u003c/td\u003e\n    \u003c/tr\u003e\n  \u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How is GEO similar to SEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"GEO has similarities with SEO, they both rely on the production of high quality, well structured content that offers value to the reader. Both disciplines also require that relevant content be published on high authority third party sources, such as the media, and trusted industry websites. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"What are the key differences between GEO and SEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"SEO is more focused on optimizing for specific keywords or search terms, while in GEO the emphasis is on creating content that is contextually relevant to the query, providing accurate and detailed information that will help give the user a useful answer. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"How does GEO impact SEO?","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"GEO does not replace SEO, and the two disciplines are complementary in many ways. For example, content that is built primarily for GEO purpose can still perform very well in conventional search. Equally, media outreach that secures positive brand coverage in trusted media channels can benefit both GEO and SEO.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Specifically, Google AI Overviews include summaries of content from top ranking search results, so in that situation strong SEO performance contributes to positive GEO.","spans":[{"start":14,"end":33,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abmYoxEAACoANQCf","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"tracking-google-ai-overview-performance-brand-monitoring-guide","first_publication_date":"2026-03-17T18:09:49+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:42:48+0000","uid":"google-ai-overview-tracking","url":"/en/blog/google-ai-overview-tracking","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"8 Essentials of Generative Engine Optimization","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"1. Benchmark and measure progress","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"The first step of any GEO strategy is to understand where you’re starting from, so use an LLM tracking tool like Meltwater GenAI Lens to audit how your brand currently appears in leading AI platforms, and benchmark against your competitors. You can then use the same tool to measure the progress of your strategy, tracking improvements and identifying continued weaknesses. ","spans":[{"start":22,"end":34,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aXEETREAACoAR_X8","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"effective-geo-strategies-that-drive-llm-visibility","first_publication_date":"2026-01-22T19:49:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-08T09:32:53+0000","uid":"geo-strategies","url":"/en/blog/geo-strategies","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":90,"end":107,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aR98BhEAACoAQjsq","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"best-llm-tracking-tools-for-marketing-teams-2026-guide","first_publication_date":"2025-11-26T22:27:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-21T12:50:29+0000","uid":"llm-tracking-tools","url":"/en/blog/llm-tracking-tools","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":113,"end":133,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}},{"start":205,"end":239,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/blog/competitor-benchmarking-what-it-is-and-why-it-is-important","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"2. Answers, not keywords","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Ask yourself this: What questions could our business be the answer to? Think of all the potential conversations a person might have with an LLM that your brand would like to be mentioned in. Map them all out, and build a content plan that provides genuinely useful answers.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"3. Messaging consistency","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"If there are conflicting facts about your brand scattered across different channels, and even inconsistencies on your own website, it will be more difficult for LLM’s to identify the truth. Build consistency into your comms strategy — wherever you talk about your brand, be sure to always get the facts straight. ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"4. Authority and evidence","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"LLMs give more weight to information that can be verified through high quality sources and credible authors. Wherever possible link to high quality third party sources that back up any claims you make in your content. Also ensure the spokespeople you attribute content to have detailed author bios on your website, and build up their profiles on third party sources including LinkedIn and industry media.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"5. Owned media ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Your website is the first place an LLM is likely to discover and absorb knowledge about your business, so make sure all of the information is constantly kept up to date and presented in clear, factual language that an AI can understand. Make sure it’s correctly structured too.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"6. Earned Media ","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Generative Engines rely heavily on authoritative independent media sources to add credibility and recency to their responses. It is therefore critical to ensure that your media relations activity is consistently placing accurate, on-message information in the press, because those stories will find their way into LLM responses.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"7. Community Engagement","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Research shows that major LLMs draw a lot of their knowledge from discussions in online communities, such as Reddit and Medium. If you don’t already know which online communities are most relevant to your business, you should start researching them now, and build a plan for how you’re going to engage authentically with them.","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Tip: Learn How to Gain AI Visibility and Reputation Across Customer Touchpoints","spans":[{"start":0,"end":79,"type":"label","data":{"label":"Content-Box"}},{"start":11,"end":79,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYYUvhEAACoAU4Uy","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-gain-ai-visibility-and-reputation-across-customer-touchpoints","first_publication_date":"2026-02-06T16:24:12+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-04-14T17:37:39+0000","uid":"ai-visibility-reputation-customer-touchpoints","url":"/en/blog/ai-visibility-reputation-customer-touchpoints","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading3","text":"8. Broad content footprint","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Most Generative Engines favor different sources when they look for more recent information to supplement their base knowledge. Some lean more towards major news sources, while others prefer social media, and it’s possible that they will update their preferences over time. So it’s important to ensure your content and messaging is seeded across a wide range of sources. You can also use GenAI Lens to identify which sources the Generative Engines are currently using.","spans":[{"start":387,"end":397,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}}],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"heading2","text":"Increase your GEO readiness with Meltwater","spans":[],"direction":"ltr"},{"type":"paragraph","text":"As generative AI transforms how audiences discover brands (AI-driven brand discovery), Meltwater helps organizations prepare for the next evolution of search, GEO. Our AI-powered platform gives you the visibility, insights, and agility needed to understand how your brand appears across LLMS like ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Perplexity. With Meltwater’s GenAI Lens, the industry’s first AI visibility tracking solution, you can see exactly how your brand, products, and competitors are represented in AI-generated answers, identifying misinformation, emerging narratives, and new opportunities for visibility before they shape public perception.","spans":[{"start":59,"end":84,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYN0exEAAC0AT3DL","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"ai-driven-brand-discovery-a-game-changer-for-marketers","first_publication_date":"2026-02-04T23:50:52+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-27T11:26:42+0000","uid":"ai-brand-discovery","url":"/en/blog/ai-brand-discovery","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":257,"end":304,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aYN5ChEAACwAT3jp","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"monitoring-brand-mentions-in-chatgpt-the-what-why-and-how","first_publication_date":"2026-02-06T17:18:27+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-05-07T07:12:23+0000","uid":"chatgpt-brand-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/chatgpt-brand-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":314,"end":320,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"acRQGBEAAC0ARbxg","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"how-to-track-brand-mentions-in-claude-ai","first_publication_date":"2026-03-26T19:38:43+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-30T11:19:36+0000","uid":"track-brand-mentions-claude","url":"/en/blog/track-brand-mentions-claude","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":326,"end":336,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"abxtlhEAACsAOWLA","type":"blog_post","tags":[],"lang":"en-us","slug":"perplexity-brand-monitoring-how-to-monitor-your-brand-mentions-on-perplexity-ai","first_publication_date":"2026-03-24T00:19:34+0000","last_publication_date":"2026-03-30T11:14:43+0000","uid":"perplexity-brand-monitoring","url":"/en/blog/perplexity-brand-monitoring","link_type":"Document","isBroken":false}},{"start":343,"end":365,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}},{"start":388,"end":419,"type":"hyperlink","data":{"id":"aR98BhEAACoAQjsq","type":"blog_post","tags":["MoFu"],"lang":"en-us","slug":"best-llm-tracking-tools-for-marketing-teams-2026-guide","first_publication_date": GenAI Lens product banner with a product screenshot","copyright":null,"dimensions":{"width":750,"height":500},"id":"aQxjt7pReVYa4Hkv","edit":{"x":0,"y":0,"zoom":1,"background":"transparent"},"linkTo":{"link_type":"Web","url":"https://www.meltwater.com/en/products/genai-lens","target":"_blank"}},{"type":"paragraph","text":"Beyond monitoring, Meltwater empowers you to act.
